By exploiting the permutation symmetry of the system, we construct a symmetry-adapted Hamiltonian that reduces the computational complexity while preserving the exact dynamics. This enables a systematic investigation of the interplay among the Rabi frequency, laser detuning, and Rydberg blockade strength, leading to the identification of optimal operating regimes with high fidelity, fast gate operation, and suppressed leakage. We further quantify the dominant error mechanisms, including phase errors, population leakage, and finite Rydberg-state lifetimes, and evaluate their impact on the gate fidelity. Our results demonstrate experimentally accessible high-fidelity gate operation and provide practical guidelines for multi-qubit quantum computing.

</details>


### [102] [Generative Replay Mitigates Sample Starvation in Quantum Architecture Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11248)
*Akash Kundu,Amit Kumar Jaiswal,Sebastian Feld,Prayag Tiwari*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出 GenQAS，一个结合矩阵乘积态热启动与优先生成式回放的张量网络引导强化学习框架，用于量子架构搜索，通过局部转移模型按需生成合成电路转移并与真实经验混合，以缓解搜索空间膨胀下的样本饥饿问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习虽可自动化量子架构搜索，但随搜索空间迅速膨胀，有效电路轨迹变得稀少，导致可扩展性受限。现有回放机制仅重用已观测转移，难以提供足够训练信号。

Method: 在随机探索分析下发现近基态电路仅占据可用状态空间中快速收缩的区域。据此设计 GenQAS：以固定矩阵乘积态（MPS）进行热启动，引入优先生成式回放，用学习到的局部转移模型从真实状态-动作种子出发按需生成单步合成转移，并在 Double Deep Q-Network 更新中与真实经验混合。

Result: 在 6–12 量子比特化学哈密顿量基准上，GenQAS 提升固定预算成功率并找到紧凑电路，能量误差具竞争力；12 量子比特时最终成功率较被动回放最高提升 7.0 倍；15 量子比特横场 Ising 模型成功率由 12% 提升至 21%；含噪 6 量子比特 BeH₂ 迁移实验中，生成式回放将达化学精度所需步数减少 92.7%。

Conclusion: 生成式回放可有效缓解量子架构搜索中的样本饥饿，支撑更资源高效的电路发现。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) can automate quantum architecture search, but its scalability is limited when useful circuit trajectories become rare in the rapidly expanding search space. Existing replay mechanisms reuse observed transitions; the proposed learned model produces additional predicted one step transitions from real state-action seeds. Here we introduce GenQAS, a tensor network-guided RL framework that combines a fixed matrix product state warm-start with prioritized generative replay. A learned local transition model generates synthetic circuit transitions on demand and mixes them with real experience during Double Deep Q-Network updates. Under a random exploration analysis, near ground state circuits occupy a rapidly shrinking region of the accessible state space. We investigate whether real data anchored synthetic replay can improve the effective training signal in this regime. Across chemical Hamiltonian benchmarks from 6 to 12 qubits, GenQAS improves fixed-budget success probability and identifies compact circuits at competitive energy error. At 12 qubits, it improves final success probability by up to $7.0\times$ over passive replay. On a 15-qubit transverse field Ising model, GenQAS increases success probability from $12\%$ to $21\%$. In a noisy 6-qubit BeH$_2$ transfer experiment, generative replay reduces the steps to chemical accuracy by $92.7\%$. These results show that generative replay can mitigate sample starvation in quantum architecture search and support more resource efficient circuit discovery.

</details>


### [103] [Virtual quantum neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11327)
*Benchi Zhao,Xuanqiang Zhao,Yinan Li,Yingzhou Li,Giulio Chiribella*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作提出"虚拟量子神经网络"，通过随机采样与经典数据处理扩大优化空间，使其涵盖完全正映射的线性组合，从而提升表达能力与抗噪性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准量子神经网络在参数化量子电路（酉算子或完全正映射）上最小化损失函数，其优化空间受限，表达能力和抗噪性不足，难以充分发挥近期待量子硬件的应用潜力。

Method: 在量子神经网络基础上引入随机采样与经典数据处理，将优化空间扩展至完全正映射的线性组合，构建"虚拟量子神经网络"模型。

Result: 在三个代表性任务上验证了所提模型的有效性：量子误差缓解、二分类以及基态能量估计；扩大的优化空间带来了更强的表达能力和更好的噪声鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 虚拟量子神经网络提供了一种灵活的学习范式，扩展了可实现的量子计算空间，并增强了近期待量子硬件的应用能力。

Abstract: Quantum neural networks are a prominent model of quantum machine learning. Their training consists in the minimization of a given loss function over a parametrized family of quantum circuits, mathematically described by unitary operators, or, more generally, completely positive linear maps. In this work, we extend the notion of quantum neural network, using random sampling and classical data processing to enlarge the optimization space in a way that includes linear combinations of completely positive maps. Our extended model, called virtual quantum neural networks, leverages its enlarged optimization space to achieve increased expressivity and improved noise robustness. These benefits are illustrated in three representative tasks: quantum error mitigation, binary classification, and estimation of ground-state energies. Overall, virtual quantum neural networks offer a flexible learning paradigm that expands the space of achievable computations and strengthens the applications of near-term quantum hardware.

</details>


### [104] [Quantum Random Access Memory Implementation Using Photon-Photon Interaction in Rydberg Atomic Ensemble](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11333)
*Avirup Chakraborty,Shrabana Chakrabarti*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种可扩展qRAM架构，用相位编码量子行走子取代主动节点，基于EIT-Rydberg原子系综与空心波导实现稳健路由，达到O(n log(n+m))时间复杂度和O(n+m)物理行走子开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: qRAM是突破量子机器学习数据加载瓶颈的关键，但现有物理实现可扩展性差：传统fanout设计需要指数级易退相干门，bucket-brigade方案依赖高错误率的主动开关。为规避这些限制，需从根本上替换主动节点。

Method: 将离散时间量子行走映射到腔量子电动力学框架，利用基于电磁诱导透明（EIT）的Rydberg原子系综；在空心波导内，借助强Rydberg偶极-偶极相互作用与螺线管磁场构造稳健路由算符，该算符施加偏振相关的精确相移，引导圆偏振探测脉冲至目标存储单元。

Result: 在强控制场区间运行可抑制涌现的空间衰减，保证高规模存储地址的累积传输概率；架构将空间资源优化为静态门，时间复杂度达到最优对数尺度O(n log(n+m))，所需物理行走子为O(n+m)。

Conclusion: 该并行化架构为先进量子计算实现提供了一条实用、容错的硬件路径。

Abstract: Quantum random access memory (qRAM) is crucial for overcoming data-loading bottlenecks in quantum machine learning; however, current physical implementations face severe scalability constraints. Traditional fanout designs demand exponential decoherence-prone gates, while bucket-brigade schemes require highly error-prone active switches. Motivated by these limitations, we propose a scalable qRAM architecture that fundamentally replaces active nodes with phase-encoded quantum walkers. Our methodology maps a discrete-time quantum walk onto a cavity quantum electrodynamics framework utilizing an electromagnetically induced transparency (EIT)-based Rydberg atomic ensemble. Inside hollow-core waveguides, strong Rydberg dipole-dipole interactions and a solenoidal magnetic field create a robust routing operator. This operator imparts precise, polarization-dependent phase shifts, steering circularly polarized probe pulses to target memory cells. Our results demonstrate that operating within a strong control field regime suppresses emergent spatial attenuation, ensuring cumulative transmission probabilities for highly scaled memory addresses. Ultimately, this parallelized architecture successfully optimizes spatial resources to static gates and temporal complexity to an optimal logarithmic scale of $\mathcal{O}(n\log(n+m))$ by requiring $\mathcal{O}(n+m)$ physical walkers, establishing a practical, fault-tolerant hardware pathway for advanced quantum computation implementations.

</details>


### [105] [Quantum-RAM Implementation Using Multiple Interacting Rydberg-Blockaded EIT Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11344)
*Avirup Chakraborty,Shrabana Chakrabarti*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出基于里德堡阻塞原子系综中量子随机行走、利用多能级EIT实现qRAM的新理论架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 克服以往固态或囚禁离子方案依赖几何相位门、需低温运行的局限。

Method: 利用里德堡原子间强相干偶极-偶极相互作用，通过多组Λ型EIT系统中的交叉相位调制产生条件相位；用塞曼分裂在腔内构建平行Λ系统，以圆偏振激光耦合基态磁子能级与高激发里德堡态，形成二元树节点；地址与数据量子比特编码于探测场并相干映射至亚稳原子态。系统由n+m层冷碱原子构成n级里德堡节点二元树，连接2^n个腔囚禁存储原子，由n+m个激光脉冲充当量子行走者与地址单元。

Result: 实现光子量子比特的高保真相位控制，无需低温；操作复杂度降至O(n)，具备可扩展性。

Conclusion: 该方案为光子qRAM提供可扩展、高相干路径，利用集体里德堡相互作用实现可编程并行纠缠操作。

Abstract: We propose a novel theoretical architecture for implementing a quantum random access memory (qRAM) based on quantum random walks in a Rydberg blockaded atomic ensemble utilizing multilevel Electromagnetically Induced Transparency (EIT). Unlike previous approaches that rely on geometric phase gates in solid-state or trapped ion systems, our scheme harnesses the strong, coherent dipole dipole interactions between Rydberg atoms to achieve high-fidelity phase control of photonic qubits without the need for cryogenic temperatures. By generating conditional phase shifts through cross-phase modulation in multiple lambda-type EIT systems, we realize the controlled unitary operations requisite for an efficient qRAM. In the proposed architecture, Zeeman splitting is used to engineer a set of parallel lambda systems in a cavity, where pairs of magnetic sublevels of the ground state are coupled to highly excited Rydberg states via circularly polarized laser pulses. These Rydberg excited EIT systems serve as the elementary phase gates that form the nodes of a binary tree enabling quantum random walking. Address and data qubits are encoded into distinct probe fields and coherently mapped into the metastable atomic states, where their interactions within the EIT medium generate conditional phases required for state-selective routing. The system uses $n+m$ layers of cold alkali atoms to form an $n$-level binary tree of Rydberg nodes connected to $2^n$ cavity-trapped memory atoms, operated by $n+m$ laser pulses acting as quantum walkers and address units. Our scheme offers a scalable, reducing operational complexity to $\mathcal{O}(n)$ and highly coherent pathway toward photonic qRAM, exploiting collective Rydberg interactions to realize programmable, parallel entangling operations in an atomic ensemble.

</details>


### [106] [Optimizing quantum encodings for analog simulation through dynamical algebra reachability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11351)
*Mariane Mangin-Brinet*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出几何无关框架，用于诊断并优化目标Hamiltonian谱代数与模拟量子器件原生控制所生成Krylov型算子空间之间的兼容性，并将其应用于Rydberg原子处理器上的氘核Hamiltonian编码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模拟量子计算机虽可直接访问连续多体动力学，但其原生控制Hamiltonian只能生成受限的算子空间。因此，器件能否复现目标Hamiltonian动力学，不仅取决于谱匹配，还取决于物理控制是否真能生成所需演化。现有方法缺乏对"代数可达性"这一表示层面兼容性的诊断工具。

Method: 构建几何无关框架：以目标Hamiltonian H生成的谱代数与由器件独立可调控制Hamiltonian及固定初态生成的Krylov型算子空间为对象，将编码问题表述为目标Hamiltonian酉轨道上的优化；在U(d)上用Riemannian梯度下降最大化光滑的子空间重叠泛函，从而选出与原生控制更对齐的谱等价表示。将该框架用于Rydberg原子模拟处理器上编码的氘核Hamiltonian，对比标准二进制编码与主角度优化。

Result: 在小系统规模下，标准二进制编码与Krylov型算子空间严重失配；主角度优化显著提升了编码的代数兼容性。此外，采用单一的几何无关优化编码可大幅降低态制备保真度对原子几何构型的敏感性。

Conclusion: 代数可达性可作为模拟编码设计的有用预处理判据：能在器件特定几何与脉冲优化之前识别表示层面的不兼容性。代数级可达性与态级可达性之间的区分，明确了何时需要完整的编码优化、何时依赖几何相关的控制资源即可补偿不完全的代数对齐。

Abstract: Analog quantum computers provides direct access to continuous many-body dynamics, but their native control Hamiltonians generate only a restricted operator space. Consequently, the fidelity with which they can reproduce a target Hamiltonian's dynamics depends not only on spectral agreement but on whether the physical controls can actually generate the required evolution. We introduce a geometry-independent framework for diagnosing and optimizing this compatibility between the spectral algebra generated by a target Hamiltonian $H$ and a Krylov-type operator space generated from the device's independently tunable control Hamiltonians and a fixed initial state. The encoding problem can be formulated as an optimization over the unitary orbit of the target Hamiltonian. We maximize a smooth subspace-overlap functional using Riemannian gradient descent on $U(d)$, thereby selecting a spectrally equivalent representation whose target algebra is better aligned with the native controls. We apply this framework to the deuteron Hamiltonian encoded on a Rydberg-atom analog processor. The standard binary encoding is strongly misaligned with this space for small system sizes, while principal-angle optimization substantially improves the algebraic compatibility of the encoding. Moreover, using a single geometry-independent optimized encoding substantially reduces the sensitivity of state-preparation fidelity to the atomic geometry. These results establish algebraic reachability as a useful preprocessing criterion for analog encoding design: it identifies representation-level incompatibilities before device-specific geometry and pulse optimization, while the distinction between algebra-level and state-level reachability clarifies when full encoding optimization is necessary and when geometry-dependent control resources can compensate for incomplete algebraic alignment.

</details>


### [107] [Engineering Quantum Links: Noise and Quantum-State-Degradation Metrics over Metropolitan Fiber Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11359)
*Marcello Caleffi,Laura d'Avossa,Angela Sara Cacciapuoti*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作在那不勒斯Federico II大学7.3公里城域光纤环上开展实验，为量子网络建立类似经典链路的信道表征基础：测量量子版SINR（暗计数为本底噪声，拉曼散射和纤间串扰为干扰）以及偏振、时间、频率三个自由度的BER及其时间漂移，表明量子光纤链路可用少量可测参数刻画，从而将量子网络从物理演示转变为工程设计问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在既有网络基础设施上部署量子网络，需要与经典通信相同的工程基础，即对信道噪声及其对传输信息所造成损伤的定量建模。然而，量子链路目前缺乏类似经典链路SINR与BER的、基于实测的量化表征框架。

Method: 在国家QuantumInternet.it测试床上，利用连接那不勒斯Federico II大学两个校区的7.3公里已部署城域光纤环进行实验测量。噪声方面，采用光子计数量子SINR类比：暗计数作为本底噪声，经典业务产生的光子（自发拉曼散射或纤间串扰）作为干扰，并分别在实际环路上量化各贡献。误码方面，针对光子在偏振、时间与频率三个编码自由度，逐一量化信道引起的性能退化及其随时间漂移。

Result: 在实际部署环路上定量给出了暗计数、自发拉曼散射与纤间串扰各自的噪声贡献；并对偏振、时间、频率三个自由度分别测得了信道诱导的BER退化及其时间漂移特性。

Conclusion: 量子光纤链路与经典链路一样，可由一小组可测参数加以刻画，这使在已部署光纤上的量子网络从物理演示转变为工程设计问题，并为量子互联网的量子链路预算提供了关键要素。

Abstract: Deploying quantum networks over existing network infrastructures requires the same engineering foundations that underpin classical communications: quantitative models of the channel's noise and of the impairments it imposes on the transmitted information. In this work, we build such a foundation on experimental measurements, grounding the quantum-network counterparts of the two cornerstone metrics of classical link characterization - namely, the SINR and the BER - on a 7.3 km deployed metropolitan-scale fiber-loop interconnecting two campuses of the University of Naples Federico II within the national QuantumInternet.it testbed. On the noise side, we adopt a photon-counting quantum analog of the SINR - in which dark counts constitute the intrinsic noise and the photons generated by classical traffic (through either spontaneous Raman scattering or inter-fiber crosstalk) constitute the interference - and we quantify each contribution directly on the deployed loop. On the bit-error side, we consider the main degrees-of-freedom available to encode a quantum state within an optical photon - namely, polarization, time, and frequency - and we quantify for each degree the channel-induced degradation and its drift over time. These results show that a quantum fiber link, like its classical counterpart, can be captured by a small set of measurable parameters, turning quantum networking over deployed fiber from a physics demonstration into an engineering design problem. Together, they provide the key ingredients of a quantum link budget for the Quantum Internet.

</details>


### [108] [Entanglement Meets Reality: A Network Engineering Assessment and Forecast of Rackable Entanglement Sources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11387)
*Laura d'Avossa,Daniela Salvoni,Angela Sara Cacciapuoti,Marcello Caleffi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文从网络工程视角评估可机架部署的即插即用纠缠光子源，提出多光子产生与纠缠质量两项KPI，并结合探测对速率与单向哈希界估计可蒸馏纠缠生成速率下界，进而推导纠缠蒸馏所需量子存储相干时间下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子网络正从实验室走向实际部署，纠缠源实质上定义了量子网络，其性能直接影响未来量子通信的可靠性、可扩展性与效率。现有工作多聚焦器件表征，缺乏面向部署的性能评估。

Method: 基于大规模实验测试，在多种工作条件下评估商用可部署的机架式即插即用纠缠光子源；定义两项KPI：多光子产生（刻画偏离理想单对发射的程度）与纠缠质量（通过重建的双量子比特密度矩阵量化）；将实测探测对速率与由密度矩阵导出的单向哈希界结合，估算可蒸馏纠缠生成速率的下界；并将实验结果转化为纠缠蒸馏所需量子存储相干时间的下界。

Result: 给出了商用可部署纠缠源在多种工况下的部署导向性能评估，识别并量化了两项关键KPI，提出了融合对吞吐与纠缠质量的紧凑度量（可蒸馏纠缠生成速率下界），并导出对未来量子中继节点量子存储相干时间的硬件要求下界。

Conclusion: 该工作将纠缠源评估从器件表征转向部署导向，为当前最优能力提供基准，并对未来纠缠源代际发展给出展望，同时把光源性能与未来量子中继节点的硬件需求直接关联。

Abstract: Quantum networks are transitioning from labo- ratory experiments to real-world deployments, with entangle- ment as their fundamental resource. Since an entanglement source effectively defines a quantum network, its performance directly impacts the reliability, scalability, and efficiency of future quantum communications. In this work, we investigate rack-mountable plug-and-play entangled-photon sources from a network engineering perspective, shifting the focus from device characterization to deployment-oriented performance evaluation. Building upon an extensive experimental campaign, we assess commercially deployable hardware across multiple operating conditions, evaluate current state-of-the-art capabilities, and pro- vide an outlook on future generations of entanglement sources. We identify and evaluate two key performance indicators (KPIs): multi-photon generation, capturing deviations from ideal single- pair emission, and entanglement quality, quantified through the reconstructed two-qubit density matrix. By combining the measured detected-pair rate with the one-way hashing bound derived from each density matrix, we estimate a lower bound on the achievable distillable-entanglement generation rate, providing a compact metric that captures the trade-off between pair throughput and entanglement quality. Finally, we translate these experimental results into lower bounds on the quantum-memory coherence time required for entanglement distillation, directly linking optical source performance to the hardware requirements of future quantum repeater nodes.

</details>


### [109] [The Quantum Plumber's Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11416)
*Nicolas G. Underwood,Holger F. Hofmann,Jonte R. Hance*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将量子反事实增益推广到"量子水管工问题"：水管工需确定干涉仪中被阻断的路径，比较多种策略并在Hofmann三路径干涉仪上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究通过Kirkwood-Dirac准概率分布负性量化了扩展Elitzur-Vaidman炸弹测试式场景中的量子反事实增益，本文希望将这一量子优势识别框架扩展到新的场景。

Method: 定义"量子水管工问题"，研究对象为广义路径编码干涉仪及Hofmann三路径干涉仪；分析多种确定受阻路径的策略；通过大量模拟实验收集数据，评估各竞争策略的相对优劣；并研究用非破坏探测器替代阻断的变体游戏。

Result: 给出多种策略的相对优劣，并通过模拟数据支持对竞争策略的判断；同时报告了阻断被非破坏探测器替代时的变体结果。

Conclusion: 量子反事实增益的分析框架可推广到量子水管工问题，为在路径阻断识别任务中确认量子优势提供了新场景与证据。

Abstract: Recent work quantified the notion of quantum counterfactual gain for an extended Elitzur-Vaidman bomb test style scenario, through a connection to the negativity of the Kirkwood-Dirac quasiprobability distribution. We here extend this work to identifying quantum advantage in a new scenario, which we term the ``Quantum Plumber's Problem''. In this scenario, we imagine a ``quantum plumber'', who knows that one path of an interferometer is blocked, and wants to find the optimal strategy for identifying with certainty which path this is. We discuss various strategies for a generalised path-encoded interferometer, as well as for the specific case of Hofmann's three-path interferometer, introduced in a recent analysis of the relationship between states in five measurement contexts of a three level system. We support our arguments on the relative merit of competing strategies with data collected over many simulated attempts at locating blockages. We also present results for a variant of the game in which the blockage is replaced by a non-demolition detector.

</details>


### [110] [Self-guided certification of nonlocality in quantum networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11451)
*Jean Cortés,Luciano Pereira,Aldo Delgado*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自引导协议，通过变分优化各方测量以最大化网络贝尔不等式的违背，结合局部泡利经典阴影高效评估与CSPSA搜索，并在三角网络中用Wagon-Wheel不等式数值验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝尔定理表明量子理论与局域隐变量模型不相容。近年非局域性研究已从原始贝尔场景扩展到量子网络：多个独立源在远距离方之间分配物理系统，产生由非线性贝尔不等式（而非标准贝尔不等式）认证的关联。然而，在网络场景中高效寻找并认证违反网络贝尔不等式的测量设置仍具挑战。

Method: 提出自引导协议：变分优化各方的测量以最大化网络贝尔不等式的违背。每步通过局部泡利经典阴影高效评估违背值，搜索由复同步扰动随机逼近（CSPSA）算法驱动。收敛后，将返回的测量设置直接实施并重新评估不等式（不使用阴影）。该两阶段结构将依赖设备的搜索与仅依赖观测统计和网络因果结构的证书分离。

Result: 在三角网络上用Wagon-Wheel不等式进行数值模拟验证：恢复了Fritz分布所达到的违背，并将认证扩展到非最大纠缠态和含噪态。

Conclusion: 该自引导协议能有效优化并认证网络贝尔不等式的违背，其两阶段设计提供了独立于设备细节的证书，适用于非最大纠缠与噪声场景。

Abstract: Bell's theorem shows that quantum theory is incompatible with local hidden-variable models. In recent years, research on nonlocality has moved beyond Bell's original scenario to quantum networks, where multiple independent sources distribute physical systems among distant parties, giving rise to correlations certified by nonlinear rather than standard Bell inequalities. Here, we introduce a self-guided protocol that variationally optimizes each party's measurement to maximize the violation of a network Bell inequality, with the violation evaluated efficiently at each step via local Pauli classical shadows and the search driven by the Complex Simultaneous Perturbation Stochastic Approximation (CSPSA) algorithm. Once converged, the measurement settings it returns are implemented directly and the inequality is re-evaluated without shadows. This two-stage structure separates a device-dependent search from a certificate that depends only on the observed statistics and on the causal structure of the network. We validate the protocol by numerically simulating it on the triangle network using the Wagon-Wheel inequality, recovering the violation achieved by the Fritz distribution, and extending the certification to non-maximally entangled and noisy states.

</details>


### [111] [Joint Mitigation of Algorithmic and Physical Errors in Noisy Hamiltonian Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11508)
*Shuo Zhou,Xinzhao Wang,Ruiqi Zhang,Xiaoyang Wang,Yi-Cong Zheng,Tongang Li,Shengyu Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出将可调噪声强度与Trotter步长耦合的一维联合外推协议，用单次Richardson外推同时消除物理噪声与Trotter误差，并在超导量子处理器上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 乘积公式哈密顿量模拟适合近期量子处理器，但精度受有限步Trotter偏差与硬件噪声两类竞争误差限制，需同时抑制二者。

Method: 令每层噪声强度随步长缩放λ(s)=c(sT)^{p+1}（p阶乘积公式），沿一条一维路径使物理噪声与Trotter领头修正同阶（s^p），用单次Richardson外推同时抵消；基于既有有限阶BCH截断界推导对易子标度的资源保证；采用学习式噪声放大，在超导量子计算机上演示Ising动力学，并用100比特SPD模拟对照。

Result: 对局域哈密顿量与局域Lindblad噪声，协议以仅常数渐进开销（相对无噪声Trotter外推）同时缓解物理噪声与Trotter误差，前提是所需噪声强度高于器件固有噪声底；实验实现Ising动力学；100比特SPD模拟以更少线路设置达到与二维Richardson外推相当的精度。

Conclusion: 通过噪声-步长联合标度与单次外推，可将硬件噪声转化为可外推的自由度，为近期量子模拟提供可证明且资源可控的误差缓解方案。

Abstract: Product-formula Hamiltonian simulation is naturally suited to near-term quantum processors, but its accuracy is set by two competing errors: finite-step Trotter bias and physical hardware noise. We introduce a joint extrapolation strategy that ties the tunable per-layer noise strength to the Trotter step size, $λ(s)=c(sT)^{p+1}$ for a $p$-th order product formula. Along this one-dimensional path, the leading physical-noise and Trotter corrections over the full evolution both enter at order $s^p$ and can be canceled by a single Richardson extrapolation. Building on a previously established finite-order Baker--Campbell--Hausdorff truncation bound, we derive a provably commutator-scaling resource guarantee for the joint extrapolation. For local Hamiltonians with local Lindbladian noise, the protocol mitigates physical noise together with Trotter error with only a constant asymptotic overhead relative to noiseless Trotter extrapolation, provided the required noise strengths lie above the intrinsic device-noise floor. We experimentally demonstrate the protocol for Ising dynamics on a superconducting quantum computer using learned noise amplification. Complementary 100-qubit Sparse Pauli Dynamics (SPD) simulations achieve comparable accuracy to two-dimensional Richardson extrapolation with fewer circuit settings.

</details>


### [112] [One-clean-qubit spectroscopy of simulated Kitaev chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11513)
*Z. M. McIntyre,Daniel Loss*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种数字-模拟量子模拟协议，利用门定义量子点中的自旋量子比特阵列，通过单自旋测量提取Kitaev链的单粒子谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kitaev链的单粒子谱（尤其是平庸-拓扑相变）包含重要凝聚态物理信息，但在真实材料中难以直接测量；量子点自旋比特平台高度可编程，适合对其建模与模拟。

Method: 经Jordan-Wigner变换将Kitaev链映射为量子比特，并在量子点线性阵列中以驱动工程实现N格点横场Ising模型（TFIM）；周期性地根据控制比特状态切换模拟参数，使控制比特动力学频闪式地匹配one-clean-qubit（DQC1）计算模型的输出，再由经典后处理从TFIM的2^N个本征能中分离出N个单粒子能；通过改变Rabi驱动强度工程合成横场。

Result: 控制比特的频闪动力学可复现DQC1输出并给出TFIM全谱；经典后处理成功提取Kitaev链的N个单粒子能量；改变Rabi驱动强度可映射出从平庸到拓扑区域的谱特征交叉。

Conclusion: 该数字-模拟量子模拟协议仅需测量单个自旋即可获取Kitaev链单粒子谱并探测其拓扑相变，为量子点自旋量子比特模拟凝聚态现象提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Spin qubits in gate-defined quantum dots provide a highly programmable platform for simulating condensed-matter phenomena. In this work, we introduce a digital-analog quantum simulation protocol for extracting the single-particle spectrum of a Kitaev chain. The Kitaev chain is mapped onto qubits via the standard Jordan-Wigner transformation and implemented as a drive-engineered, $N$-site transverse-field Ising model (TFIM) in a linear array of quantum dots. We show that periodically toggling the analog-simulation parameters conditioned on the state of a control qubit causes the dynamics of this control qubit to stroboscopically match the output of the one-clean-qubit (DQC1) model of computation, thereby yielding the full spectrum of the TFIM from measurements of a single spin. Classical postprocessing can then be used to isolate the $N$ single-particle energies of the Kitaev chain from the $2^N$ eigenenergies of the TFIM. By varying the strength of the Rabi drive used to engineer the synthetic transverse field, the spectral signature of the crossover from the trivial to the topological regime of the Kitaev chain could then be mapped out with measurements of just one spin.

</details>


### [113] [Birth and Death of Entanglement in Hamiltonian-Driven Quantum Games under Decoherence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11522)
*Teena Thomas,S. Balakrishnan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究了幅度阻尼退相干下基于横场Ising模型（TFIM）的两玩家量子博弈，分析噪声对纳什均衡、收益、并发度与相干性的影响，发现退相干逐步抑制量子策略优势，最大阻尼时所有结果退化为相同的经典收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子博弈理论研究量子资源对策略决策的影响，但实际量子系统不可避免存在退相干；本文旨在探究幅度阻尼噪声如何影响基于TFIM的量子博弈的策略与关联特性。

Method: 构建基于横场Ising模型（TFIM）的两玩家量子博弈框架；TFIM哈密顿量天然产生一族纠缠门，为量子博弈提供物理上合理的实现；在幅度阻尼退相干信道下，针对不同初态与策略对，分析纳什均衡、玩家收益、并发度（concurrence）及量子态相干性的变化。

Result: 退相干会逐步抑制量子策略优势，当阻尼达到最大时，所有博弈结果都退化为彼此相同的经典收益；对博弈中所生成量子态的并发度与相干性分析显示，量子关联动力学依赖初态与策略选择，并出现纠缠突然产生与突然消亡（entanglement sudden birth and death）现象。

Conclusion: 幅度阻尼退相干会削弱乃至消除TFIM量子博弈中的量子策略优势，其程度由噪声强度决定；量子关联（纠缠与相干性）随初态和策略呈现非平凡动力学，是理解噪声环境下量子博弈表现的关键因素。

Abstract: Quantum game theory investigates the influence of quantum resources on strategic decision-making. In this work, two-player quantum games based on the Transverse Field Ising Model(TFIM) are investigated under amplitude-damping decoherence. The TFIM Hamiltonian naturally produces a family of entangling gates, enabling a physically motivated implementation of quantum games. The effects of noise on Nash equilibria, players' payoffs, concurrence and coherence of the quantum states for different initial states and strategy pairs are analyzed. The results show that decoherence progressively suppresses quantum strategic advantages, with maximum damping driving all outcomes to identical classical payoffs. The concurrence and coherence analysis of the states generated in the quantum game reveal initial state and strategy dependent quantum correlation dynamics, including entanglement sudden birth and death.

</details>


### [114] [Impossible to conjugate an unknown quantum state via a unitary evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11536)
*Dafa Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出"不可共轭定理"（no-conjugating theorem）：对未知任意量子态，不存在普适物理装置或线性/幺正操作能够实现共轭变换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子信息中存在一系列"不可能定理"（不可克隆、不可删除、不可隐藏、不可翻转、不可广播），它们刻画了量子力学的根本限制。本文旨在将这一不可能性框架推广到"共轭"这一操作，填补该系列定理的空白。

Method: 以类比已有不可能定理的方式，对"普适量子共轭"操作进行理论分析，考察其能否由普适物理装置或线性/幺正操作实现，并给出不可共轭定理的表述。

Result: 证明了不可共轭定理：不存在普适物理装置或线性/幺正操作，能够对未知的任意量子态执行共轭变换。

Conclusion: 不可共轭定理与不可克隆、不可删除、不可隐藏、不可翻转、不可广播等定理并列，构成量子信息不可能定理族的新成员，进一步揭示量子操作的普适性限制。

Abstract: The no-cloning theorem, the no-deleting theorem, the no-hiding theorem, the no-flip theorem, and the no-broadcasting play a crucial role in quantum mechanics and quantum information. In this paper, we propose the no-conjugating theorem which states that it is impossible to conjugate an unknown arbitrary quantum state via a universal physical device or linear/unitary operation.

</details>


### [115] [Dissipative Quantum Battery from Many-Body Scars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11595)
*Jinglin Liu,Yiming Liu,Mingdi Xu,Lei Pan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出基于耗散选择的量子多体疤痕的自主量子电池协议，利用局域键耗散将系统驱动到高能疤痕流形，实现接近热力学功界限的可提取功并保持相干结构，弱局域退相位可缩短充电时间，疤痕破坏会同步损失储能性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索量子多体疤痕作为量子能量存储资源的可能性，寻找自主、鲁棒的量子电池机制，并验证优良电池性能与疤痕非热结构的关联。

Method: 采用具有精确疤痕塔的嵌入型自旋链和广义相互作用 J1–J2 链；引入工程化局域键耗散驱动系统从被动态进入高能疤痕支撑流形；再施加弱局域退相位以稳定充电协议并消解非衰减边缘模式；最后引入明确的疤痕破坏微扰作为对照。

Result: 充电态的单副本遍历功（ergotropy）接近熵匹配的热力学功界限，同时保留疤痕阶梯的相干结构；弱退相位下存在宽广参数区间，可在不明显降低存储功的情况下缩短充电时间；疤痕破坏微扰导致疤痕支撑与可提取功的关联性损失。

Conclusion: 耗散稳定的量子多体疤痕可作为自主且鲁棒的量子能量存储资源，其电池性能源于非热疤痕结构而非一般激发态的制备。

Abstract: We propose an autonomous quantum-battery protocol based on dissipatively selected quantum many-body scars. Using an embedding-type spin chain with an exact scar tower and a generalized interacting $J_1$--$J_2$ chain, we show that engineered local bond dissipation can drive the system from a passive state into a high-energy scar-supported manifold with large extractable work. The resulting charged states exhibit single-copy ergotropy close to the entropy-matched thermodynamic work bound while retaining the coherent structure associated with the scar ladder. Because the ideal scar manifold also supports nondecaying peripheral modes, we introduce weak local dephasing to obtain a stable charging protocol and find a broad regime in which the charging time is reduced without substantially degrading the stored work. Finally, an explicit scar-breaking perturbation produces a correlated loss of scar support and extractable work, demonstrating that the favorable battery performance is tied to the nonthermal scar structure rather than merely to the preparation of a generic excited state. Our results establish dissipatively stabilized many-body scars as a promising resource for autonomous and robust quantum energy storage.

</details>


### [116] [Quantum models of interaction Hamiltonian and their paradoxes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11618)
*Nicolas Gisin,Serge Massar,Jef Pauwels,Pavel Sekatski*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究能按设计遵循光锥并重现两体哈密顿量的微观量子模型，定量分析微观中介体对远程系统造成的残余效应（稳态修饰、快速本地控制下的退相干），并说明这些模型如何化解超光速通信与局域能量核算等悖论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子物理中常用多体哈密顿量描述远程系统相互作用，这种"超距作用"式描述若按字面理解会引发与超光速通信相关的悖论及局域能量核算的不一致，同时忽略了远程系统与实现相互作用的中介体之间的纠缠、以及远程系统进行局域演化时产生的退相干等残余效应。

Method: 构建按设计遵循光锥、并能重现两体哈密顿量的简单微观量子模型，定量分析微观中介体对远程系统产生的残余效应，包括稳态的修饰（dressing）以及在快速局域控制下的退相干。

Result: 在这些模型中定量刻画了微观中介体引入的残余效应，包括稳态修饰与快速局域控制下的退相干，并展示了模型如何化解前述悖论。

Conclusion: 遵循光锥的微观模型能够重现两体哈密顿量的有效描述，同时揭示并量化中介体带来的残余效应，从而化解超距作用描述所导致的超光速通信和局域能量核算悖论。

Abstract: In quantum physics it is commonplace to model the interaction of remote systems with a many-body Hamiltonian. Taking such an action-at-a-distance description {\it à la lettre} leads to various paradoxes related to faster-than-light communication and apparent inconsistencies in local energy accounting. It also neglects residual effects, such as entanglement between the remote systems and the mediator that implements the interaction, or the decoherence that arises when the remote systems undergo local evolution. We study simple microscopic quantum models that respect the light cone by design and reproduce two-body Hamiltonians. For these models we quantitatively analyze the residual effects of the microscopic mediator on the remote systems, including dressing of stationary states, and decoherence in the presence of fast local control. We show how the models resolve the paradoxes.

</details>


### [117] [Quantum-hardware spectral co-design framework for multifrequency Rydberg electrometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11635)
*Song Huang,Guibin Tian,Fernando Quijandría,Zhengjiang Li,Zhimin He,Franco Nori,Yong Lu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出将受制造约束的SPSA-Adam优化器直接耦合到全波有限元本征模仿真，用于多频里德堡电场测量的量子-硬件频谱协同设计。该方法在约10^300的离散设计空间中优化，使四个高Q本征模同时对准四个选定的Cs里德堡跃迁，结构鲁棒且器件长度大幅缩短。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子系统对电磁硬件提出离散的、由量子定义的频谱约束，如何设计满足这些约束的硬件构成一个普遍的频谱协同设计问题。多频里德堡电场测量中，传统方法难以实现多模同时频谱匹配。

Method: 将受制造约束的同步扰动随机逼近（SPSA）-Adam优化器与全波有限元本征模仿真直接耦合，无需解析梯度或伴随梯度，在约10^300个离散构型的设计空间中进行优化。

Result: 得到一种新型多模电场测量架构，四个高Q本征模与四个选定Cs里德堡跃迁同时对准；相对频率误差低至8.42×10^-7，器件长度缩短1.75×10^2倍，对制造缺陷鲁棒；相同仿真预算下，频率匹配误差分别比CMA-ES和离散模拟退火小约10倍和41倍；平均输入功率灵敏度提升约4.04×10^3。

Conclusion: 该工作证明量子-硬件频谱协同设计是通向多通道量子传感紧凑硬件的一条通用途径，克服了传统设计同时多模频谱匹配的困难。

Abstract: Engineering electromagnetic hardware to satisfy discrete quantum-defined spectral constraints constitutes a general spectral co-design problem for quantum systems. Here we address this challenge in multifrequency Rydberg electrometry by directly coupling a fabrication-constrained simultaneous perturbation stochastic approximation (SPSA)--Adam optimizer to full-wave finite-element eigenmode simulations. Requiring neither analytical nor adjoint gradients, the method operates over a discrete design space containing approximately $10^{300}$-configurations and yields a novel multimode electrometry architecture that simultaneously aligns four high-$Q$ eigenmodes with four selected Cs Rydberg transitions. The optimized design remains highly robust to fabrication imperfections, achieving a relative frequency error as low as $8.42\times10^{-7}$ while reducing the device length by a factor of $1.75\times10^{2}$, thereby overcoming the difficulty of simultaneous multimode spectral matching encountered in conventional design. For a comparable simulation budget, the proposed co-design framework achieves frequency-matching errors approximately 10 and 41 times smaller than those of covariance matrix adaptation evolution strategy and discrete simulated annealing, respectively. The electrometry is predicted to provide an average input-power-sensitivity enhancement of approximately $4.04\times10^{3}$, demonstrating quantum--hardware spectral co-design as a general route toward compact hardware for multichannel quantum sensing.

</details>


### [118] [Certifying Adversarial Robustness of Quantum Classifiers under Known-Readout Query Access](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11637)
*Ji Guan,Mingyu Huang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究已知读出查询(KRQA)下的量子分类器认证对抗鲁棒性,提出仅依赖测量与采样结果的框架,同时给出下界(排除半径内非定向错误)与上界(见证半径内对抗态),无需层析或电路描述,并具备有限样本保证及真实硬件可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子分类器通过演化量子态并测量输出标签,重复执行仅得到标签分布,因此鲁棒性难以直接审计。在KRQA设定下,评估者可准备输入、已知测量、观测有限次采样结果,但无法检查内部演化、参数或梯度,需要一种仅基于可观测统计的认证方法。

Method: 提出仅测量(measurement-only)框架,对每个输入返回两类互补保证:半径内排除非定向错误的下界,以及见证对抗态的上界。二者均可由已知读出测量与采样结果估计,无需 tomography 或电路描述,并给出有限样本保证。上界利用量子测量诱导的 gap 算子;下界将状态空间搜索松弛为在算子谱约束下对结果分布的高效优化。

Result: 在多个量子分类器上评估:下界在可处理实例上追踪精确最优;上界在标准攻击失效时仍具信息量。在 IBM Quantum 硬件上,用两个 8-qubit 量子神经网络各 40 次执行,方法计算出两种证书,且每个测试输入上下界均呈现预期顺序。

Conclusion: 结果表明,在 KRQA 下量子分类器的鲁棒性主张可直接从可观测统计中审计;所得证书不弱于既有仅概率证书,且当谱约束起作用时可严格更强。

Abstract: A quantum classifier assigns labels by evolving an input quantum state and measuring the output, so repeated executions reveal only a distribution over labels. We study certified adversarial robustness for such classifiers under known-readout query access (KRQA), where an evaluator can prepare inputs, knows the quantum measurement, and observes finite-shot outcomes but cannot inspect the internal evolution, parameters, or gradients. We give a measurement-only framework that returns two complementary guarantees for each input: a lower bound ruling out untargeted errors within a radius, and an attack-independent upper bound witnessing an adversarial state within a radius. Both are estimable from the known readout measurement and sampled outcomes, require no tomography or circuit description, and admit finite-sample guarantees. The upper bound uses gap operators induced by the quantum measurement; the lower bound relaxes state-space search to an efficient optimization over outcome distributions with operator-spectrum constraints, yielding certificates that are never weaker than prior probability-only certificates and can be strictly stronger when the spectral constraints are active. Evaluations on multiple quantum classifiers show that the lower bound tracks exact optima on tractable instances, while the upper bound remains informative when standard attacks fail. We further demonstrate real-device feasibility on IBM Quantum hardware: from 40 executions of two 8-qubit quantum neural networks, our method computes both certificates, with the expected ordering between the lower and upper bounds on every tested input. Taken together, these results show that robustness claims for quantum classifiers can be audited directly from observable statistics under KRQA.

</details>


### [119] [A Near-Quartic Separation Between Certificate Complexity and Quantum Query Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11664)
*Andris Ambainis,Jānis Iraids,Martins Kokainis*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 构造了一个总布尔函数 $f$，满足 $Q(f)=\widetilde{O}(\sqrt[4]{C(f)})$，解决了量子查询复杂度与证书复杂度关系的长期开放问题，且结果在忽略对数因子意义下紧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究有界错误量子查询复杂度 $Q(f)$ 与证书复杂度 $C(f)$ 之间的最优分离关系，此前缺乏达到 $Q(f)=\widetilde{O}(\sqrt[4]{C(f)})$ 的构造，该比值是否为最优长期未决。

Method: 显式构造一个总布尔函数 $f$，通过设计其证书结构使其证书复杂度较大，同时利用量子算法实现仅需 $\widetilde{O}(\sqrt[4]{C(f)})$ 次查询的有界错误求解，从而在两者之间建立新的上界关系。

Result: 得到总布尔函数满足 $Q(f)=\widetilde{O}(\sqrt[4]{C(f)})$，并证明该界在忽略对数因子意义下是紧的。

Conclusion: 该构造给出了 $Q(f)$ 与 $C(f)$ 之间已知最紧的分离，宣告相关长期开放问题得到解决。

Abstract: We construct a total Boolean function $f$ for which $Q(f) = \widetilde{O}(\sqrt[4]{C(f)})$, where $Q(f)$ denotes the bounded-error quantum query complexity and $C(f)$ denotes the certificate complexity. This resolves a longstanding open question and is tight up to logarithmic factors.

</details>


### [120] [Lifted surgery: Fast processing with QLDPC codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11723)
*Lucas Berent,Lawrence Z. Cohen,Armanda O. Quintavalle*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出"lifted surgery"方法，通过在阿贝尔群代数码上保持码的对称性，实现快速并行的逻辑测量，显著降低QLDPC码逻辑操作的时间开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QLDPC码是实现低开销容错量子计算的重要候选，但其逻辑操作的时间开销仍是关键挑战。传统code surgery通过反复的syndrome测量轮次引入此开销，需要一种既保持低物理开销又加快逻辑操作速度的方法。

Method: 提出lifted surgery方法，作用于阿贝尔群代数码。利用块分解与交换代数技术表征lifted surgery，研究性质良好的子族并构造具体实例，构造出量子radial码[[90,8,10]]和[[198,8,16]]。

Result: 所构造的量子radial码支持快速、并行且可寻址的surgery，可在单轮syndrome提取中测量同Pauli类型的任意独立逻辑算符集合。在电路级去极化噪声基准测试下，[[90,8,10]]码的逻辑性能与标准code surgery相当，但所需syndrome测量轮次减少十倍。

Conclusion: lifted surgery兼具速度与并行性，为低开销容错量子计算提供了一条切实可行的路径。

Abstract: Quantum low-density parity-check (QLDPC) codes are a leading candidate for achieving low-overhead fault-tolerant quantum computing. However, the time overhead of logical operations in QLDPC codes remains a key challenge. Code surgery, a space-efficient technique for fault-tolerant logical measurements, incurs this overhead through repeated rounds of syndrome measurement. We introduce lifted surgery, a method for fast and parallel surgery on Abelian group algebra codes that maintains the low physical overhead that makes QLDPC codes attractive. Lifted surgery preserves the symmetries of the underlying code, making searches for large instances tractable and offering a natural route towards efficient hardware implementations. We utilise block decompositions and techniques from commutative algebra to characterise lifted surgery, investigate well-behaved subfamilies, and construct explicit examples. In particular, we present quantum radial codes with parameters $[[90, 8, 10]]$ and $[[198, 8, 16 ]]$ for which surgery is fast, parallel, and addressable, allowing arbitrary sets of independent logical operators of the same Pauli type to be measured in a single round of syndrome extraction. We benchmark lifted surgery under circuit-level depolarising noise and, for the $[[ 90, 8, 10 ]]$ code, find logical performance comparable to standard code surgery while requiring ten times fewer rounds of syndrome measurement. By combining speed and parallelism, lifted surgery offers a practical route towards low-overhead fault-tolerant quantum computing.

</details>


### [121] [An exchange-assisted entangling gate between 87Rb and 171Yb Rydberg atoms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11730)
*Han Wang,Erdong Huang,Mingrui Jing,Hongshun Yao,Xin Wang,Jinguo Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在双物种$^{87}\mathrm{Rb}$–$^{171}\mathrm{Yb}$光镊阵列中，识别了零电场下的光学可及$S{+}S\leftrightarrow P{+}P$ Förster共振，实现种间强偶极-偶极交换，并借助整形光脉冲演示了0.36 μs的交换辅助受控-Z门，本征保真度达99.91%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双物种中性原子阵列可将长寿命Yb核自旋数据量子比特与快速、物种选择性可控的Rb辅助比特相结合，具备可扩展量子信息处理潜力。但二价原子中实现种间门时易诱发破坏性的Stark混合，如何在不引入该问题的前提下完成种间门操作仍是未解难题。

Method: 识别零电场条件下光学可及的$S{+}S\leftrightarrow P{+}P$ Förster共振，在阵列间距下获得强偶极-偶极交换相互作用；在有限控制响应条件下采用整形光学脉冲驱动交换辅助的受控-Z门，并分析有界扰动下的鲁棒性；同时识别辅助的排斥性van der Waals通道。

Result: 实现0.36 μs的交换辅助受控-Z门，本征保真度为99.91%；在有界扰动下保真度仍保持在99.85%以上；并识别出可作为补充工具的排斥性van der Waals通道。

Conclusion: 该工作为双物种中性原子阵列实现快速、高保真种间纠缠门提供了可行途径，避免二价原子的破坏性Stark混合，并通过Förster交换通道与van der Waals通道构成面向混合量子处理器的综合工具箱。

Abstract: Neutral-atom tweezer arrays support scalable quantum information processing. Dual-species $^{87}\mathrm{Rb}$--$^{171}\mathrm{Yb}$ arrays combine long-lived ytterbium nuclear-spin data qubits with fast, species-selective rubidium ancilla control and readout. However, realizing interspecies gates without inducing destructive Stark mixing in divalent atoms remains an outstanding problem. Here, we identify an optically accessible $S{+}S\leftrightarrow P{+}P$ Förster resonance at zero electric field, providing strong dipole-dipole exchange at array pitch. Using a shaped optical pulse under finite control response, we demonstrate a $0.36\,μ\mathrm{s}$ exchange-assisted controlled-$Z$ gate with an intrinsic fidelity of $99.91\%$, remaining above $99.85\%$ under bounded perturbations. We also identify an auxiliary repulsive van der Waals channel, providing a comprehensive toolbox for hybrid quantum processors.

</details>


### [122] [Quantum transport along a tight-binding chain connected to Markovian reservoirs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11741)
*P. S. Muraev,A. R. Kolovsky,D. N. Maksimov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究边界耦合马尔可夫库且具均匀在位退相的非相互作用粒子在一维紧束缚链中的量子输运，用主方程方法给出链上平均电流的精确解析解，并进一步给出玻色多体解与电流分布及关联函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为刻画开放量子系统中退相环境对非相互作用粒子输运的影响，并在单粒子框架下得到可同时适用于玻色子和费米子的精确电流表达式，同时扩展到玻色多体情形以获取电流涨落与关联信息。

Method: 采用主方程（master equation）方法，处理两端耦合马尔可夫库、链上均匀在位退相的紧束缚模型；在单粒子量子力学框架下求解平均电流的精确解析解；在无相位阻尼时，将结果推广为玻色问题的完整多体解——反正规序特征函数表示。

Result: 得到沿链平均电流的精确解析解，该解对非相互作用玻色子与费米子同样适用；无相位阻尼情形下得到玻色多体反正规序特征函数，并据此计算链中各键电流的分布函数以及键间电流关联函数。

Conclusion: 该工作为耗散紧束缚链中非相互作用粒子的稳态量子输运提供了精确可解析描述，单粒子解统一覆盖玻色/费米情形，多体特征函数进一步给出电流统计与时空关联。

Abstract: We consider quantum transport of non-interacting particles in a tight-binding chain coupled to Markovian reservoirs at its boundaries and subject to uniform on-site dephasing. Using the masterequation approach, we derived the exact analytic solution for the mean current along the chain. This analytic solution is obtained in the framework of single-particle quantum mechanics and, thus, equally applies for transport of non-interacting bosons and fermions. In the absence of phase damping, the results are extended to the complete many-body solution for bosonic problem in the form of anti-normally ordered characteristic function. This function allows us to calculate the distribution function for the current at each bond in the chain as well as the inter-bond current correlation functions.

</details>


### [123] [Strong Converse for Quantum Capacity via a Fully Quantum Blowing-Up Lemma](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11771)
*Salman Beigi,Marco Tomamichel*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了有限维无记忆量子信道在量子容量之上的指数强逆定理：任何高于量子容量的固定速率下，编码的纠缠传输保真度随信道使用次数指数衰减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子通信中量子容量的强逆定理此前尚不完整，需要刻画超过量子容量时保真度衰减的速度，以完善信道编码理论的极限刻画。

Method: 证明包含两个主要工具：(1) 完全量子blowing-up引理，将低保真度码转换为高保真度码，其传输量子比特数的损失由信道Stinespring膨胀像上正交投影在接收方-环境二分下的投影张量范数控制；(2) 低次多项式构造，以指数精度逼近该投影的张量幂并控制其投影范数，为blowing-up论证提供所需近似。

Result: 证明了对于任意有限维无记忆量子信道，当速率高于量子容量时，每个码的纠缠传输保真度随信道使用次数指数衰减，即建立了量子通信的指数强逆定理。

Conclusion: 该工作完整刻画了超过量子容量时量子通信保真度的指数衰减行为，确立了量子信道的指数强逆定理，所发展的完全量子blowing-up引理与低次多项式逼近方法具有独立的技术价值。

Abstract: We prove an exponential strong converse for quantum communication through every finite-dimensional memoryless channel: at any fixed rate above the quantum capacity, the entanglement-transmission fidelity of every code decays exponentially with the number of channel uses. The proof has two main ingredients. First, a fully quantum blowing-up lemma converts a low-fidelity code into a high-fidelity code, with a loss in the number of transmitted qubits controlled by the projective tensor norm of the orthogonal projection on the image of the Stinespring dilation of the channel across the receiver--environment bipartition. Second, a low-degree polynomial construction approximates the tensor power of this projector with exponential accuracy while controlling its projective norm, providing the approximation needed for the blowing-up argument.

</details>


### [124] [Absolutely Maximally Entangled States of $2q$ Parties in Every Odd Prime-Power Dimension $q$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11796)
*Mykhailo Hontarenko,Karol Życzkowski*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 对每个奇素数幂 $q=p^e\ge3$，构造了稳定子 $\mathrm{AME}(2q,q)$ 态及其单方投影所得的 $\mathrm{AME}(2q-1,q)$ 态，并给出导出 MDS 码与 Hadamard 矩阵的显式构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 绝对最大纠缠（AME）态对应完美张量与最优量子纠错码，但已知存在性仅局限于有限的参数区间，因此需要拓展其存在范围并给出显式构造。

Method: 利用 $\mathbb{F}_{q^2}$ 上闭式 $q\times q$ 边框循环矩阵 $A_q$ 生成 Hermitian 自对偶 MDS 码 $[2q,q,q+1]_{q^2}$；通过归一化单方投影由 $\mathrm{AME}(2q,q)$ 得到 $\mathrm{AME}(2q-1,q)$；并在适当基下把振幅张量写成阶为 $q^q$、相位为 $p$ 次根的归一化 $q$-酉复 Hadamard 矩阵，结合子矩阵重缩放构造中间粒子数族。

Result: 对所有奇素数幂 $q=p^e\ge3$ 构造出稳定子 $\mathrm{AME}(2q,q)$ 与 $\mathrm{AME}(2q-1,q)$ 态；所得 MDS 码不属于扩展 Reed-Solomon 类；给出 $q$-酉复 Hadamard 矩阵以及 $\mathrm{AME}(q+3,q)$ 态和中间粒子数族，并列出九个显式母矩阵及相应单方投影。

Conclusion: 该工作以自对偶 MDS 码为核心工具，系统扩展了 AME 态的已知参数范围，并提供可显式验证的构造与实例。

Abstract: Absolutely maximally entangled (AME) states represent an extreme form of multipartite entanglement: every reduced system containing at most half of the parties is maximally mixed. These states provide perfect tensors and optimal quantum error-correcting codes, yet their existence is known only in restricted parameter regimes. For every odd prime power $q=p^e\ge3$, we construct a stabilizer $\mathrm{AME}(2q,q)$ state whose normalized one-party projection yields a stabilizer $\mathrm{AME}(2q-1,q)$ state. A closed-form $q\times q$ bordered-circulant matrix $A_q$ over $\mathbb{F}_{q^2}$ generates a Hermitian self-dual maximum distance separable (MDS) code $[2q,q,q+1]_{q^2}$, which lies outside the extended Reed-Solomon classes. In suitable bases, the amplitude tensors define normalized $q$-unitary complex Hadamard matrices of order $q^q$ with $p$th-root phases. Additional constructions yield $\mathrm{AME}(q+3,q)$ states and families at intermediate particle numbers through explicit rescalings of selected submatrices. We also provide nine explicit parent matrices and the corresponding one-party projections.

</details>


### [125] [Oracle Separations in the Fourier Hierarchy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11830)
*Atul Mantri*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文证明了 Fourier 层级相邻层级之间的相对化严格分离：对每个常数 k≥2，存在一个 oracle 使 FH_k ⊊ FH_{k+1}。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Shi（TCS 2005）引入的 Fourier 层级 FH_0⊆FH_1⊆…，按 Hadamard 层数衡量量子计算能力，并猜想每增加一层都会严格增强计算能力，同时提出首先需要相邻层级的 oracle 分离。该问题在每一级 k≥2 上此前均未解决。

Method: 分离问题基于 Forrelation（Aaronson 与 Ambainis, STOC 2015）：上一层用常数次查询即可求解，而第 k 层即使允许指数多次查询仍难解。构造同时适用于相位 oracle 与标准 oracle 两种访问模型；下界基于层级结构性质——Hadamard 层数限制了电路对 oracle 查询的自适应性，相位 oracle 下 k 层电路可被仅有 k−1 轮并行查询的算法精确复现，从而借用已知下界；标准 oracle 允许依据早期答案分支，需单独论证。

Result: 对每个常数 k≥2，存在 oracle 使 FH_k ⊊ FH_{k+1}；该分离对相位 oracle 和标准 oracle 均成立。两种 oracle 不可互换：相对某 oracle，相同层数下标准 oracle 严格更强。此外还相对 oracle 分离了所有层级的并集与 BQP。

Conclusion: 该工作首次在所有层级 k≥2 上给出 Fourier 层级的 oracle 分离，验证了 Shi 猜想的第一步，并揭示了 oracle 访问方式对该层级结构的实质影响。

Abstract: The Fourier hierarchy $\mathrm{FH}_0\subseteq\mathrm{FH}_1\subseteq\mathrm{FH}_2\subseteq\cdots$, introduced by Shi (TCS 2005), measures a quantum computation by the number of Hadamard layers it uses. Between two layers the circuit may permute basis states and attach phases, but it may not create superposition; the layers are its only source of interference. The first level is exactly $\mathrm{BPP}$, while the second already solves Simon's problem and, through phase estimation, factors integers. Shi conjectured that every additional layer strictly increases computational power, and asked, as a first step, for oracle separations between consecutive levels. To our knowledge, the question was open at every level $k\ge2$.
  We prove that for every constant $k\ge2$ there is an oracle relative to which $\mathrm{FH}_k\subsetneq\mathrm{FH}_{k+1}$. The separating problem is built from Forrelation (Aaronson and Ambainis, STOC 2015): the level above solves it with a constant number of queries, whereas at level $k$ it stays hard even for circuits making exponentially many queries. This holds for both of the usual ways of giving a circuit access to an oracle, the phase oracle and the standard oracle, which writes its answer into a register. The two are not interchangeable: relative to an oracle, the standard oracle is strictly more powerful at the same number of layers. We also separate the union of all the levels from $\mathrm{BQP}$ relative to an oracle.
  The lower bounds rest on a structural property of the hierarchy: the number of Hadamard layers limits how adaptively a circuit can query its oracle. With a phase oracle, a circuit with $k$ layers is reproduced exactly by an algorithm making only $k-1$ rounds of parallel queries, which brings known lower bounds for such algorithms to bear. The standard oracle lets a circuit branch on earlier answers, and that case needs a separate argument.

</details>


### [126] [The cost of simulating classically tractable quantum circuits and dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11847)
*Su Yeon Chang,Supanut Thanasilp,Zoë Holmes,M. Cerezo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对经典可模拟的量子动力学，比较其在经典计算机上与在量子硬件上执行的资源效率，使用量子采样、量子时间与经典时间三个度量，发现多项式时间可模拟性本身不足以决定首选实现方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多项式时间可模拟性只是渐近性陈述，无法说明经典模拟在实践中是否真正实用；不同的多项式标度可能导致计算成本差异巨大，尤其是当需要昂贵的预处理或量子数据采集时。因此需要研究经典可模拟的量子动力学在实践中究竟是经典模拟更省资源，还是直接在量子硬件上执行更省资源。

Method: 针对若干广泛研究的经典可模拟电路族，采用量子采样复杂度、量子时间复杂度和经典时间复杂度三个资源度量进行分析，并使用具有代表性的硬件层面估计进行对比。

Result: 识别出一些区域：尽管存在多项式时间经典算法，量子模拟仍可能更快；同时也存在经典模拟更高效的区域。此外，表征未知输入态所需的大量量子采样成本，使得在当前云硬件访问价格下，这种多项式时间经典模拟可能过于昂贵。

Conclusion: 仅凭多项式资源下经典可模拟性的保证，不足以确定更优的实现方式；需要结合具体资源度量和实际硬件成本进行综合评估。

Abstract: Determining whether a quantum evolution can be efficiently simulated classically is central to understanding the boundary between classical and quantum computation. However, polynomial-time simulability is an asymptotic statement, and does not by itself determine whether the (quantum-inspired) classical simulation is actually practical. Indeed, different polynomial scalings can lead to vastly different computational costs, particularly when expensive preprocessing or quantum data acquisition is required. In this work, we ask whether classically simulable quantum dynamics are in practice more resource-efficient to simulate classically than to execute directly on quantum hardware. We analyze this question using three resource metrics, quantum sample, quantum time, and classical time complexity, for several widely studied classically simulable circuit families. Using representative hardware-level estimates, we identify regimes in which quantum simulation can be faster despite the existence of a polynomial-time classical algorithm, as well as regimes in which classical simulation remains more efficient. At the same time, the large quantum sampling cost needed to characterize unknown input states can make this polynomial-time classical simulation prohibitively expensive with current cloud-based hardware access prices. Ultimately, our work indicates that guarantees of classical simulability with polynomial resources alone are insufficient to determine the preferred implementation.

</details>


### [127] [PPT states of almost maximal Schmidt number](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11849)
*Nathaniel Johnston,Benjamin Lovitz*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文构造了局部维数较小的 $\mathbb{C}^m\otimes\mathbb{C}^n$ 上的 PPT 态，其 Schmidt 数渐近达到较小局部维数；当 $m=n$ 时可达到 $n-\lfloor(2n-1)^{1/2}\rfloor$，远超此前 $n/2+O(1)$ 的构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究 PPT 态的 Schmidt 数（纠缠维数）上界问题。此前构造在等维情形仅达到 $n/2+O(1)$，本文希望突破这一水平，逼近较小局部维数的渐近极限。

Method: 在 $\mathbb{C}^m\otimes\mathbb{C}^n$ 上显式构造 PPT 态，并分析其 Schmidt 数下界，给出具体解析表达式：$\left\lceil \frac{m+n-\sqrt{(m-n)^2+4(m+n-1)}}{2} \right\rceil$。等维与非等维情形分别处理。

Result: 等维 $m=n$ 时 Schmidt 数至少为 $n-\lfloor(2n-1)^{1/2}\rfloor$，显著优于此前 $n/2+O(1)$；非等维时，在 $\mathbb{C}^n\otimes\mathbb{C}^{3n-4}$ 上存在 Schmidt 数至少 $n-1$ 的 PPT 态。

Conclusion: 所构造的 PPT 态使 Schmidt 数渐近逼近较小局部维数，改进了已知下界，表明 PPT 判据在高维情形下的局限性比此前认识更强。

Abstract: We construct PPT states on $\mathbb{C}^m \otimes\mathbb{C}^n$ that have Schmidt number asymptotically approaching the smaller local dimension. More specifically, we construct a PPT state with Schmidt number at least $$ \left\lceil \frac{m + n - ((m - n)^2 + 4(m + n - 1))^{1/2}}{2} \right\rceil. $$ In the case of equal local dimensions ($m = n$), this becomes $n - \lfloor(2n - 1)^{1/2}\rfloor$, far exceeding the previous constructions which achieved $n/2 + O(1)$. In the case of unequal local dimensions, our result shows that there exists a PPT state on $\mathbb{C}^n \otimes \mathbb{C}^{3n-4}$ with Schmidt number at least $n-1$.

</details>


### [128] [PureSuperQMA(exp) = BellPureSymQMA(poly) = QMA via Dimension-Free Bosonic Argmax](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11854)
*William Gay,Fernando Granha Jeronimo,Lenny Liu,Itai Leigh,Pei Wu,Haochen Xu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明量子复杂度类发生塌缩：$\mathsf{QMA} = \mathsf{PureSuperQMA} = \mathsf{PureSuperQMA}(\text{exp}) = \mathsf{BellPureSymQMA}(\text{poly})$。核心技术是一个关于对称张量态的"无维度"稳定性界，通过多项式多个见证寄存器结合随机对SWAP测试与置换不变提升，完成模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯态一致性（pure-state consistency）问题自然导出量子证明系统，即单一纯见证需同时满足多个接受约束。对应的类 $\mathsf{PureSuperQMA}$ 此前被认为介于 $\mathsf{QMA}$ 与 $\mathsf{QMA}(2)$ 之间，Kamminga 与 Rudolph（ITCS'26）猜想两个包含关系都是严格的。本文旨在检验并推翻这一猜想，厘清该类与其推广形式的真实复杂度地位。

Method: 核心是对称张量态的无维度稳定性界（dimension-free stability bound）。模拟使用多项式多个见证寄存器，将随机对SWAP测试与原始验证过程的置换不变提升（permutation-invariant lift）相结合。关键步骤是证明在对称子空间上，极值验证值接近某个张量幂见证（tensor-power witness）的验证值，且误差与维度无关。将该论证分别应用于两个验证模型，即得两个模拟。

Result: 证明了复杂度塌缩 $\mathsf{QMA} = \mathsf{PureSuperQMA} = \mathsf{PureSuperQMA}(\text{exp}) = \mathsf{BellPureSymQMA}(\text{poly})$，即原猜想中的两个严格包含均不成立。作为推论，对于每个固定的 $k\ge2$，精确 $k$-局域纯态一致性问题是 $\mathsf{QMA}$-完全的；相应的精确玻色子与费米子纯 $N$-可表示性（$N$-representability）问题也是 $\mathsf{QMA}$-完全的。

Conclusion: $\mathsf{PureSuperQMA}$ 及其允许指数多检查的变体并不强于 $\mathsf{QMA}$，二者相等，从而否定了Kamminga–Rudolph的严格包含猜想。该塌缩也确立了精确 $k$-局域纯态一致性以及玻色/费米纯 $N$-可表示性问题的 $\mathsf{QMA}$-完全性。

Abstract: Pure-state consistency problems naturally lead to quantum proof systems in which a single pure witness must satisfy many acceptance constraints. The corresponding class $\mathsf{PureSuperQMA}$ was previously known to lie between $\mathsf{QMA}$ and $\mathsf{QMA}(2)$, and Kamminga and Rudolph (ITCS'26) conjectured that both containments are strict. In this paper, we prove the following surprising complexity collapses $$
  \mathsf{QMA} = \mathsf{PureSuperQMA} = \mathsf{PureSuperQMA}(\text{exp}) = \mathsf{BellPureSymQMA}(\text{poly}) $$ Here $\mathsf{PureSuperQMA}(\text{exp})$ allows exponentially many checks which are uniformly indexed and efficiently generated, while requiring an inverse-polynomial violation margin and an inverse-polynomial fraction of violated checks for the NO cases. $\mathsf{BellPureSymQMA}(\text{poly})$ is a related model that requires the prover to give the verifier polynomially many copies of a pure state, which the verifier measures separately with logarithmic output length for each local measurement, before processing the outcomes jointly.
  The main technical ingredient is a dimension-free stability bound for symmetric tensor states. Our simulations use polynomially many witness registers and combine a random-pair SWAP test with a permutation-invariant lift of the original verification procedure. The key step is to show that, on the symmetric subspace, the extremal verification value is close to that of some tensor-power witness with dimension-independent error. Applying this argument to the two verification models yields both simulations. As a consequence, exact $k$-local pure-state consistency is $\mathsf{QMA}$-complete for every fixed $k\ge2$, and so are the corresponding exact bosonic and fermionic pure $N$-representability problems.

</details>


### [129] [Taking Advantage of Noise in Distributed Random Quantum Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11898)
*J. Montes,F. Borondo,Gabriel G. Carlo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文用简化二阶矩转移矩阵理论研究分布式随机电路中Pauli二阶矩在振幅阻尼、退极化与退相位噪声下的演化，将噪声谱分为径向分支与角向分支，提出弱噪声判据，并分析多核架构中的噪声辅助Haar混合条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 噪声会让随机量子电路"看起来更快"，但并不必然提升其幺正动力学的随机性；在模块化处理器中，局部门只随机化单个核心，稀缺的核间通信需将随机性扩散到整个器件，因此需要明确区分"有用的噪声随机化"与"单纯的耗散"。作者希望建立一个统一框架来刻画噪声在不同通道、拓扑和核心划分下对分布式随机电路随机化能力的影响。

Method: 作者针对受振幅阻尼、退极化和退相位噪声影响的分布式随机电路，构建了Pauli二阶矩的简化二阶矩转移矩阵理论。核心方法是将含噪谱分解为两个分支：描述非恒等Pauli权重耗散损失的径向分支，以及描述幸存非平凡子空间内Haar类混合的角向分支。基于此分离，推导出弱噪声判据：噪声只有在更强烈地压制纵向Bloch分量而非横向平面时，才对角向随机化有益。对多核架构，推导径向泄漏的普适一阶定律，并针对不同噪声通道、拓扑与核心划分对角向分支进行数值追踪。

Result: 在三类噪声通道中，该判据仅选择振幅阻尼为局部有利情形；退极化噪声为中性；退相位噪声则被径向损失主导。多核架构中，径向泄漏服从普适一阶定律，角向分支的数值追踪显示出存在真正噪声辅助Haar混合的狭窄窗口，其中振幅阻尼最为明显，但总体上排除了噪声带来通用加速的可能。

Conclusion: 该框架能够区分有用的噪声随机化与单纯的耗散。噪声辅助的Haar混合仅在有限参数窗口内出现，不具备普适性；振幅阻尼是最具潜力的通道，而退极化与退相位分别表现为中性或受耗散主导。这为模块化量子处理器中利用噪声进行随机化提供了理论判据与设计指引。

Abstract: Adding noise can make a random quantum circuit look faster without making its unitary dynamics more random. This distinction is especially relevant in modular processors, where local gates randomize each core and scarce inter-core communication must spread that randomness across the full device. In this paper, we study this problem with a reduced second-moment transfer-matrix theory for Pauli second moments in distributed random circuits affected by the amplitude-damping, depolarizing, and dephasing noise channels. The key step is to resolve the noisy spectrum into two branches: a radial branch, describing dissipative loss of non-identity Pauli weight, and an angular branch, describing Haar-like mixing within the surviving nontrivial sector. This separation gives a simple weak-noise criterion: noise is useful for angular randomization only when it suppresses the longitudinal Bloch component more strongly than the transverse plane. Among the three channels considered, this selects amplitude damping as the only locally favorable case, while depolarizing noise is neutral and dephasing is dominated by radial loss. For multicore architectures, we derive a universal first-order law for radial leakage and track the angular branch numerically across different channels, topologies, and core partitions. The results reveal narrow windows of genuine noise-assisted Haar mixing, most clearly for amplitude damping, but rule out a generic speed-up by noise. The framework therefore distinguishes useful noisy randomization from mere dissipation.

</details>


### [130] [EFI Pairs Without One-Way Puzzles: Oracle Separations from Communication Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11901)
*Atul Mantri*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在经典预言机下构造出一对EFI（相对该预言机存活）与单向谜题（相对该预言机不存在）的分离，表明单向谜题并不蕴含于EFI对，且在该预言机下量子多项式时间对任何经典输入输出任务无优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EFI对与单向谜题是量子密码学最小假设的两个主要候选。已知单向谜题蕴含EFI对，但反向是否成立（即EFI对是否蕴含单向谜题）尚属开放问题。本文旨在通过预言机分离技术，证明二者不等价，并厘清量子计算在经典输入输出任务上的优势边界。

Method: 构造单一经典预言机：该预言机对所有关于量子采样器输出概率的问题作答（从而消除单向谜题，即使验证者无界），同时隐藏一个Haar随机半维子空间（使EFI对存活于仅在最后做一次叠加查询、其余全程经典查询并持有建议的区分器）。安全性归约到通信复杂度：将对手关于子空间的知识（以经典查询答复形式到达）在双方协议中模拟，使其不优于Vector-in-Subspace（Klartag-Regev, STOC 2011）的最优经典协议，且与预言机具体计算无关。对无法覆盖的最后一次叠加查询，改用随机矩阵理论工具进行界估计。

Result: 构造出该经典预言机，相对它：单向谜题不存在（即使验证者无界），而一对EFI仍能抵抗经典查询加建议、仅在最后进行一次叠加查询的区分器。同一攻击还给出无预共享纠缠的经典消息协议中任意量子参与方的经典模拟，故相对该预言机不存在量子性证明。存在一类区分器使EFI对保持不可区分。

Conclusion: 单向谜题并不蕴含EFI对（至少在相对化意义下），二者作为最小假设不等价；且相对该预言机，量子多项式时间在任何经典输入输出任务上均无优势，而两个量子态仍不可区分。作者提出关于去除叠加查询限制的猜想。

Abstract: EFI pairs (Brakerski, Canetti, and Qian, ITCS 2023) and one-way puzzles (Khurana and Tomer, STOC 2024) are the leading candidates for the minimal assumption of quantum cryptography. The first are efficiently preparable quantum states, statistically far yet computationally indistinguishable; the second are classical puzzles, easy to sample and hard to solve. One-way puzzles imply EFI pairs, and whether the converse holds is open.
  We construct a single classical oracle relative to which one-way puzzles do not exist, even with an unbounded verifier, while an EFI pair survives every distinguisher that queries the oracle classically throughout and holds advice about it, making its one superposition query at the end. The oracle answers every question about the output probabilities of quantum samplers, which removes the puzzles, and hides a Haar-random half-dimensional subspace.
  To prove security we reduce it to communication complexity. An adversary whose knowledge of the subspace arrives as classical query answers can be simulated inside a two-party protocol against the party holding it, so it does no better than the best classical protocol for Vector-in-Subspace (Klartag and Regev, STOC 2011), whatever the oracle computes. That argument does not cover the superposition query, which we bound instead using tools from random matrix theory.
  The same attack gives a classical simulation of any quantum party in a classical-message protocol with no entanglement shared in advance, so relative to the oracle there is no proof of quantumness either. Quantum polynomial time therefore offers no advantage on any task with classical inputs and outputs, while the two quantum states stay indistinguishable. We state conjectures on removing the restriction on superposition queries.

</details>


### [131] [A Chip-scale Space-time Multiplexed Gaussian Boson Sampling Processor Beyond 10,000 Photons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11922)
*Yu-Xuan Fu,He-Yu Shen,Ke-Ming Hu,Jun-Jie He,Yun-Long Nie,Hang Song,Bao-Jing Liu,Le-Si Yang,Xiao-Yu Wu,Pei-Lin Du,Yu-Ze Zhu,Yi Xie,De-Hui Huang,Yu-Fei Liu,Hai Yan,Jin-Hong Chen,Yu-Lin Yu,Chuan-Yan Peng,Wen-Hao Zhou,Feng Lu,Yu-Quan Peng,Chen-Shuo Xia,Zhi-Chao Wang,Zhe-Han Li,Lin Chen,Chao-Yang Zhang,Chun-Yan Jin,Yan-Rong Zong,Xiao-Yun Xu,Jian-Peng Dou,Xiao-Tian Fang,Pei-Qi Zhou,Hao Tang,Chang-Shun Wang,Lin Yang,Xian-Min Jin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文报道首个芯片级时空复用高斯玻色采样（GBS）系统，基于薄膜铌酸锂芯片单片集成高速电光调制器、片上延迟线与时空复用干涉网络，实现4 GHz时钟、1毫秒内最多11,059个光子探测事件，并进一步将硬件重构为GBS驱动的世界模型，在物理动力学建模上以更少可训练读出参数取得比经典回声状态网络更低的预测误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GBS系统面临光学对准严苛、相位不稳定、可编程性有限等工程障碍，难以规模化部署；芯片级时空复用架构有望突破这些限制，但需要晶圆级芯片同时满足低损耗、高精度和高速调制等严格要求。

Method: 在薄膜铌酸锂芯片上单片集成高速电光调制器、片上延迟线以及时空复用干涉网络，构建芯片级时空复用GBS系统，以4 GHz时钟运行，并可将光子硬件重构为GBS驱动的世界模型用于物理动力学建模。

Result: 系统在1毫秒内实现最多11,059个光子探测事件；作为GBS驱动的世界模型，相比经典回声状态网络（ESN）基线，以更少可训练读出参数获得更低的预测误差。

Conclusion: 结果验证了面向可扩展光子量子硬件这一方向的可行性，并为未来GBS量子系统的多功能可编程应用开辟了道路。

Abstract: Gaussian boson sampling (GBS) has emerged as a leading photonic paradigmfor demonstrating quantum computational advantage. Nevertheless, state-ofthe-art GBS setups face practical barriers including stringent optical alignment, phase instability, and limited programmability, which impede scalable engineering deployment. The chip-scale space-time multiplexed architecturepromises to resolve these constraints, yet it strongly demands wafer-scale chipcapabilities to simultaneously satisfy stringent requirements on low loss, highprecision and high-speed modulation. Here we report the first chip-scale spacetime multiplexed GBS system, monolithically integrating high-speed electrooptic modulators, on-chip delay lines, and a time-space multiplexed interferometric network on a thin-film lithium niobate chip, operating at a 4-GHz clockrate with detection events of up to 11,059 photons within 1 millisecond. Beyond benchmarking quantum advantage, we further reconfigure the photonichardware into a GBS-powered world model for modelling physical dynamics,which achieves lower prediction error with fewer trainable readout parameters compared with a classical echo state network (ESN) baseline. Our resultsvalidate the feasibility of our endeavor towards scalable photonic quantumhardware, and pave the way for the versatile programmable applications offuture GBS quantum systems.

</details>


<div id='hep-ph'></div>

# hep-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [132] [Nonthermal Solar Stalling of an Inelastic Scalar Signal in Xenon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10636)
*XinXin Qi,Hao Sun*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个无反常的U(1)_{B-3L_τ}模型，用于解释LUX-ZEPLIN实验在248 keV处观测到的一个核反冲候选事件：840 GeV复标量发生劈裂为320 keV的吸热跃迁，160 MeV暗矢量介导，给出0.958个可接受事件与Ωh²=0.1200。随后通过能量、角动量和内态的演化计算，得到抑制的太阳俘获与湮灭率，说明太阳中微子对电弱非弹性暗物质的限制不能模型无关地推广到轻mediator标量实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LUX-ZEPLIN扩展能量搜索报告了一个248 keV核反冲候选事件，需要构建能同时满足该信号、正确遗迹丰度与现有各类限制（太阳中微子、IceCube、NA64等）的暗物质模型，并检验太阳中微子对电弱非弹性暗物质的限制是否可模型无关地推广到含轻mediator的标量实现。

Method: 构建无反常U(1)_{B-3L_τ}模型：840 GeV复标量发生劈裂320 keV的吸热跃迁；160 MeV暗矢量（暗荷Q_χ=1）介导，利用公开LZ响应重建计算事件接受数，标量湮灭给出遗迹丰度。采用物理核子比f_n/f_p=-0.86985抑制太阳铁俘获，得到C_⊙=1.09×10¹⁹ s⁻¹。进一步在能量、角动量和内态空间中演化被俘获粒子，包含有限碰撞率、热核速度和激发态衰变。

Result: 在公开LZ响应重建下给出0.958个可接受事件；标量湮灭给出Ωh²=0.1200；太阳俘获率C_⊙=1.09×10¹⁹ s⁻¹，分布停滞于等效半径r_eff=0.194–0.198 R_⊙，对应湮灭率Γ_ann=(1.33–1.38)×10¹⁵ s⁻¹，低于模型特定IceCube公开数据代理超过三个数量级；该基准点也低于专用NA64 B-L限制。

Conclusion: 太阳中微子对电弱非弹性暗物质的限制不能模型无关地推广到轻mediator的标量实现；该基准点同时满足LZ候选事件解释、遗迹丰度及IceCube、NA64等限制，表明限制的适用性依赖于具体模型实现。

Abstract: The LUX-ZEPLIN experiment has reported one nuclear-recoil candidate at $248\kev$ in an extended-energy search. We construct an anomaly-free $U(1)_{B-3L_τ}$ model in which an $840\gev$ complex scalar undergoes an endothermic transition with splitting $320\kev$. A $160\mev$ vector with dark charge $Q_χ=1$ gives $0.958$ accepted events in a public LZ-response reconstruction while scalar annihilation yields $Ωh^2=0.1200$. The physical nucleon ratio $f_n/f_p=-0.86985$ suppresses solar iron capture, leaving $C_\odot=1.09\times10^{19}\,{\rm s}^{-1}$. We then evolve captured particles in energy, angular momentum, and internal state, including finite collision rates, thermal nuclear velocities, and the excited-state decay. The distribution stalls at an equivalent radius $r_{\rm eff}=0.194$--$0.198\rsun$, for which $Γ_{\rm ann}=(1.33$--$1.38)\times10^{15}\,{\rm s}^{-1}$, below a model-specific IceCube public-data proxy by more than three orders of magnitude. The benchmark also lies below the dedicated NA64 $B-L$ limit. Thus solar-neutrino bounds on electroweak inelastic dark matter do not transfer model-independently to light-mediator scalar realizations.

</details>


### [133] [Implications of Inelastic Dark Matter for Primordial Dark-Star Evolution: Kinematic Thresholds and Nonthermal Capture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10662)
*Qinxun Li,Shengyi Liu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究内热非弹性暗物质在原生暗星中的俘获过程，发现其相较多弹性俘获具有两个定性新特征：俘获仅在恒星越过致密度阈值后开启，且速率随致密度二次增长；非热束缚轨道粒子可形成持久储库而非热核。储库会在状态改变碰撞链末端急剧致密化并停滞，仅靠百分级半径收缩才能重启弛豫，因此非弹性俘获无法补充热湮灭核心。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LUX-ZEPLIN近期报告248 keV核反冲候选事件，重新激发了对内热非弹性暗物质的兴趣，因此有必要考察其在原生暗星中的俘获行为。传统暗星演化基于弹性俘获图像，需检验非弹性俘获带来的差异及其对暗星演化的影响。

Method: 将内热非弹性俘获动力学引入原生暗星演化框架，定义由恒星质量、暗物质-核约化质量和质量劈裂决定的运动学半径R_kin；追踪完整的态改变碰撞链，分析非热束缚轨道粒子的空间分布与弛豫过程；比较激发态即时衰变与长寿命两种情形。

Result: 内热俘获仅当恒星因吸积增长越过致密度阈值后开启，速率在阈值以上呈二次增长；新俘获粒子虽通常处于非热束缚轨道，但内热运动学可使该暂态群体空间延展，成为持久储库。态改变碰撞链末端储库急剧致密化后停滞，原因是处于低于致密度相关轨道能量的基态粒子在全星内均无允许的上散射通道；百分级半径收缩可重启弛豫。这些运动学结果不依赖于激发态是即时衰变还是长寿命。

Conclusion: 内热非弹性俘获不能补充热湮灭核心，而是形成演化的轨道分布，建立恒星与暗物质核心、储库之间的协同演化动力学。相关协同演化框架将在配套论文中展开。

Abstract: The recent 248-keV nuclear recoil candidate reported by LUX-ZEPLIN has renewed interest in endothermic inelastic dark matter, motivating us to examine its capture in primordial dark stars. Relative to the conventional elastic-capture picture in dark star evolution, endothermic capture adds two qualitative features. First, it opens only after the growing star crosses a compactness threshold, expressed as a kinematic radius $R_{\rm kin}\proptoμ_χM_\star/δ$ set by the stellar mass, the dark-matter--nucleus reduced mass and the mass splitting. The accreting growth carries the star through the threshold, and the capture rate turns on quadratically above it. Second, although newly captured particles generically begin on nonthermal bound orbits, endothermic kinematics can keep this normally transient population spatially extended, turning it into a persistent reservoir rather than an intermediate step toward a thermal core. Following complete chains of state-changing collisions, we find that the reservoir compacts sharply and then stalls, because a ground state particle below a compactness-dependent orbital energy has no allowed up-scatter anywhere in the star. A percent-level radius contraction reopens the relaxation. These kinematic results do not depend on whether the excited state decays promptly or is long-lived. As a result, inelastic capture does not replenish a thermal annihilation core. The captured population forms an evolving orbital distribution that sets up the co-evolutionary dynamics between the star and the dark matter in the core and the reservoir, which we develop in a companion paper.

</details>


### [134] [The Dark Dimension and Majorana Neutrinos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10668)
*Arturo de Giorgi,Dhruv Pasari*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于Swampland纲领提出的介观"暗维度"（尺寸0.1–10 μm、体Majorana费米子1–10 keV），推导了体Majorana中微子的地面实验约束，并给出无中微子双β衰变的有效Majorana质量的闭式表达。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Swampland纲领的最新进展暗示存在尺度为0.1–10 μm的介观暗维度，其中体Majorana费米子质量在1–10 keV范围。作者希望检验这一模型能否通过现有地面实验（中微子振荡、β衰变、无中微子双β衰变）加以约束。

Method: 确定Kaluza-Klein质量谱与混合结构后，将模型与Daya Bay中微子振荡数据、KATRIN β衰变上限以及KamLAND-Zen无中微子双β衰变限制进行对比；对后者推导有效Majorana质量的闭式表达，并分析KK塔对无中微子双β衰变的屏蔽效应；此外还考察非暗维度假设下层级化Majorana质量对约束的削弱作用。

Result: 当核动量超过紧致化尺度与Majorana尺度时，KK塔有效屏蔽无中微子双β衰变。在暗维度窗口内，β衰变给出最强约束，将允许的Yukawa耦合压低至O(10^-3)。振荡与无中微子双β衰变搜索互为补充，分别探测更小和更大的Majorana质量。

Conclusion: 暗维度中的体Majorana中微子受到现有地面实验的显著约束：β衰变主导小Majorana质量区域，振荡实验与无中微子双β衰变分别探测更小与更大质量区间；层级化Majorana质量可弱化约束。

Abstract: Recent developments in the Swampland program motivate the existence of a mesoscopic Dark Dimension of size $0.1-10~μ$m with bulk Majorana fermions in the $1-10$ keV range. Motivated by this, we derive terrestrial constraints on Majorana bulk neutrinos as a function of the compactification radius. After determining the mass spectrum and mixing structure, we confront the model with Daya Bay neutrino-oscillation data, the KATRIN beta-decay bound, and the KamLAND-Zen neutrinoless double beta decay limit. For the latter, we obtain a closed-form expression for the effective Majorana mass and show that the Kaluza-Klein tower efficiently screens neutrinoless double beta decay when the nuclear momentum exceeds the compactification and Majorana scales. In the Dark-Dimension window, beta decay provides the strongest constraint, pushing the allowed Yukawa couplings down to $\mathcal{O}(10^{-3})$. Oscillation and neutrinoless double beta decay searches remain complementary, probing smaller and larger Majorana masses, respectively. For completeness, outside the Dark-Dimension assumptions, we investigate how hierarchical Majorana masses can weaken the constraints.

</details>


### [135] [Ultralight Axial Dark Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10671)
*Anson Hook,Junwu Huang,Kevin Zhou*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 超轻暗物质可能是轴矢量场；其与费米子的轴向流或暗电偶极矩耦合带来比轴子更强的自旋力矩，而"轴子-光子"类比耦合需光子质量，主要探针为偏振测量、超导腔电场和宇宙微波背景，结论同样适用于Kalb-Ramond两形式暗物质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提出一种简单且尚未被充分探索的超轻暗物质候选者——轴矢量场，以激励新的实验探测。

Method: 理论分析轴矢量场与费米子的轴向流耦合和暗电偶极矩耦合，以及其类比轴子-光子耦合（在要求光子质量的前提下）；并利用有质量轴矢量与有质量二形式的对偶性推广结论。

Result: 轴矢量耦合产生比轴子暗物质更强的自旋力矩；在轴子-光子型耦合下背景磁场效应被压低，最强实验探针为偏振测量、超导腔中的电场和宇宙微波背景。

Conclusion: 轴矢量暗物质提供一类有前景且实验可探测的超轻暗物质方案，其结果通过二形式对偶性同样适用于弦论动机下的Kalb-Ramond暗物质。

Abstract: Ultralight dark matter could be an axial vector field, a simple possibility which motivates new experiments. Couplings to fermions, through an axial vector current or a dark electric dipole moment operator, lead to enhanced spin torques compared to axion dark matter. An axial vector also has an analogue of the axion-photon coupling, though its consistent realization requires a photon mass. Under this "axial-photon" coupling, the effect of a background magnetic field is suppressed, and the strongest experimental probes involve polarimetry, electric fields in superconducting cavities, and the cosmic microwave background. Since a massive axial vector is dual to a massive two-form, our results also apply to "Kalb-Ramond" dark matter motivated by string theory.

</details>


### [136] [On the Numerical Integration of One-Loop Cosmological Collider Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10673)
*Michael Borinsky,Aidan Herderschee,Qianshu Lu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出"缩减Schwinger方法"数值算法，首次完整计算了任意质量与大范围运动学下的单圈暴胀双谱（气泡图与三角图），并据此构建模板与CMB数据比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在广泛暴胀模型中，主导非高斯双谱来自单圈而非树图过程，但现有的单圈研究多局限于最简单的气泡图，未考虑一般质量与耦合下的三角图贡献——后者未必被压低，因此对观测而言不充分，亟需完整单圈计算。

Method: 先用Witten-Feynman参数化将de Sitter圈积分化为由Symanzik图多项式支配的广义Euler-Mellin积分；针对重质量时指数变复数导致的符号问题，引入缩减Schwinger方法，将高度振荡的子积分解析求出，得到Gauss超几何核，剩余积分用标准数值求积处理。

Result: 通过复现树级双谱的已知解析${}_3F_2$结果验证了算法；随后应用于一般运动学下的单圈气泡图与三角图，首次给出在整个运动学范围内有效的完整单圈双谱数值结果；并基于此构造双谱模板，与CMB数据进行比较。

Conclusion: 该数值方法解决了重质量下de Sitter圈积分的符号与振荡难题，实现了任意外动量与质量下单圈暴胀双谱的完整数值计算，为单圈非高斯性的观测检验提供了可靠工具与模板。

Abstract: In a broad class of inflationary models, the leading non-Gaussian bispectrum arises from one-loop rather than tree-level processes. However, realistic one-loop contributions remain largely unexplored for phenomenologically relevant masses beyond the simplest bubble diagrams. This is insufficient for observational purposes because, for generic masses and couplings, triangle contributions need not be suppressed relative to their bubble counterparts. We present a numerical method for evaluating scalar one-loop in-in diagrams contributing to the inflationary bispectrum at fully general external momenta and masses. First, the Witten-Feynman parameterization reduces de Sitter loop integrals to generalized Euler-Mellin integrals governed by Symanzik graph polynomials. Unfortunately, the exponents of the polynomials in the integrand become complex for sufficiently heavy masses, leading to sign problems when evaluating the integral using standard numerical techniques. To remedy this problem, we introduce the reduced Schwinger method, which evaluates a highly oscillatory subintegral analytically to obtain a Gauss hypergeometric kernel, leaving the remaining integrals to standard numerical quadrature. We validate the algorithm by reproducing known analytic results for the tree-level bispectrum in terms of ${}_3F_2$ functions, and then apply it to the one-loop bubble and triangle contributions at general kinematics. This yields the first direct numerical evaluation of the complete one-loop bispectrum, valid across the full kinematic range. Using these numerical results, we construct bispectrum templates and compare them with CMB data.

</details>


### [137] [Re-examining the sensitivity of JWST to decaying axion dark matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10674)
*Caleb Gemmell,Christopher Dessert,Andrea Caputo,Joshua W. Foster*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对 JWST 对 eV 级 QCD 轴子暗物质衰变光子线的灵敏度争议，采用定制数据约简与灵活非参数推断，给出稳健限制，并证明此前最强声称灵敏度超出数据本身可达上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: eV 级 QCD 轴子若构成全部暗物质，应在红外波段产生 JWST 可观测或近可观测的光子线，更一般的 ALP 亦然。但现有分析对轴子耦合灵敏度分歧达一个数量级（对应原始通量密度灵敏度相差数个量级），且与先前预测是否一致不明，亟需澄清这些矛盾。

Method: 采用定制（bespoke）数据约简流程与灵活的非参数推断方法，对公开 JWST 数据进行分析，以获得受控且稳健的 eV 级轴子暗物质衰变限制；并对比此前各分析及预测的灵敏度。

Result: 所得限制与先前预测灵敏度一致；证明此前声称的最强灵敏度超出从相应数据中任何分析可达成的上限。基于 NIRSpec 数据，排除了质量 500 meV 至 2.5 eV 的 QCD 轴子暗物质；在 100 meV 至 500 meV 范围对 ALP 暗物质给出与其他天体物理限制互补的界限。

Conclusion: 当前灵敏度受限具有系统性根源，除非仪器建模与数据约简管线大幅改进，持续数据采集或重新分析都难以提升灵敏度。

Abstract: An eV-scale QCD axion comprising the observed dark matter (DM) abundance is expected to generate a photon line at infrared energies that would be observable or near-observable in data collected by the James Webb Space Telescope (JWST), as might more general axion-like particles (ALPs) over a broader range of masses and couplings. This has motivated a number of efforts to either forecast JWST sensitivities to a QCD axion or realize them through analyses of publicly available datasets. At present, no consensus exists; leading analyses disagree by as much as an order of magnitude in terms of axion-coupling sensitivity, implying an orders-of-magnitude discrepancy in raw flux density sensitivity, and consistency between the analyses and prior forecasts is unclear. We address these outstanding discrepancies with a bespoke data reduction and flexible nonparametric inference procedure that lead to well-controlled and robust limits on the decay of eV-scale axion DM, consistent with previously forecasted sensitivities. We further demonstrate that the strongest previously claimed sensitivities exceed those attainable by any analysis of the datasets from which they were derived. We exclude QCD axion DM for masses between $500\,\mathrm{meV}$ and $2.5\,\mathrm{eV}$ using NIRSpec data, while setting limits on ALP DM complementary to other astrophysical constraints at masses between $100\,\mathrm{meV}$ and $500\,\mathrm{meV}$. However, we find the sensitivities to be systematically limited, and therefore unlikely to be improved upon by ongoing data collection or re-analysis unless instrumental modeling and data reduction pipelines improve considerably.

</details>


### [138] [Computationally Efficient Description of Medium Response to Jets in Heavy Ion Collisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10679)
*Jorge Casalderrey-Solana,José Guilherme Milhano,Daniel Pablos,Krishna Rajagopal,Xiaojun Yao*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了高效尾迹（Efficient Wake）方法，利用线性化流体力学与模板解，快速计算重离子碰撞中喷注尾迹产生的强子分布，兼顾纵向膨胀与横向径向流效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重离子碰撞中高能部分子穿过夸克胶子等离子体时沉积能量与动量，产生流体响应（喷注尾迹），影响喷注与喷注子结构可观测量的软成分。Hybrid Model 中旧的简化方法对尾迹强子分布描述不佳，而全（3+1）维非线性流体力学计算成本过高，难以用于蒙特卡洛分析。

Method: 该方法利用线性化流体力学的线性叠加性质，采用 Bjorken 流背景（无横向流）下的线性化流体力学模板解，模板只需计算一次；通过适当的旋转与助推（boost）将模板中的涨落映射到冻结面上某点的涨落，从而纳入径向流对尾迹演化及冻结面粒子产生的影响，同时考虑纵向膨胀与横向径向流。

Result: 与全（3+1）维非线性流体力学结果对比，二者符合程度合理；相比 Hybrid Model 中旧的过度简化方法，新方法对喷注尾迹来源强子分布的描述显著更好；计算效率上至少比全非线性流体力学快数万倍。

Conclusion: Efficient Wake 方法兼顾精度与效率，将其应用于重离子碰撞喷注的蒙特卡洛分析（如 Hybrid Model）后，有望显著改善对众多喷注及喷注子结构可观测量的软成分与实验数据的符合程度。

Abstract: We develop an Efficient Wake procedure for computing the distribution of hadrons originating from jet wakes in heavy ion collisions - the hydrodynamic response of a droplet of quark-gluon plasma to the energy and momentum deposited in it by high-energy partons propagating through it. The procedure employs the linearity of linearized hydrodynamics and takes account of the effects of both longitudinal expansion and transverse radial flow on the hydrodynamic evolution of the wakes and on the resulting particle production at the freezeout hypersurface. It makes repeated use of template solutions to linearized hydrodynamics in a Bjorken flow background with no transverse flow, templates that need only be computed once, and uses suitable rotations and boosts to map fluctuations from these templates to fluctuations at a point on the freezeout hypersurface in a way that incorporates the effects of the radial flow.
