### [168] [The First Correction Term in the Asymptotic Expansion of Bohr--Sommerfeld Lagrangian States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10909)
*Yusaku Tiba*

Main category: math.SG

TL;DR: 该论文研究紧Kähler流形上Bohr–Sommerfeld拉格朗日子流形对应Lagrangian states的渐近展开，显式计算首个非平凡修正项，并给出其L²范数的二阶渐近公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lagrangian states的渐近展开是几何量子化与半经典分析中的核心问题，但以往结果多停留在首项（主项）层面，缺乏对高阶修正项的显式几何刻画；本文旨在弥补这一空白，给出首个非平凡修正项的精确表达。

Method: 作者考虑装备全纯预量子线丛的紧Kähler流形及其中的紧Bohr–Sommerfeld拉格朗日子流形Λ，对与Λ关联的Lagrangian states作渐近展开，通过显式计算提取首个非平凡修正项，并将其表示为环境Kähler流形与Λ的几何不变量。

Result: 作者显式算出首个非平凡修正项，证明其由环境Kähler流形和拉格朗日子流形的标量曲率、第二基本形式及平均曲率等几何不变量表达；作为推论，得到Lagrangian states的L²范数的二阶渐近公式。

Conclusion: 该工作将Lagrangian states的渐近分析从首项推进到二阶，揭示了高阶修正项与曲率、第二基本形式、平均曲率等几何量之间的定量联系，为几何量子化及半经典对应提供了更精细的工具。

Abstract: Let $Λ$ be a compact Bohr--Sommerfeld Lagrangian submanifold of a compact Kähler manifold equipped with a holomorphic prequantum line bundle. We study the asymptotic expansion of the Lagrangian states associated with $Λ$. In particular, we compute explicitly the first nontrivial correction term and show that it is expressed in terms of geometric invariants of the ambient Kähler manifold and the Lagrangian submanifold, including their scalar curvatures, the second fundamental form, and the mean curvature. As a consequence, we obtain the corresponding second-order asymptotic formula for the $L^2$-norm of the Lagrangian states.

</details>


<div id='math-ph'></div>

# math-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [169] [A proof of the Hodge universality conjecture at nonzero dispersion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11469)
*Francisco Hernández Iglesias*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 标量τ-对称哈密顿形变由首个哈密顿密度中若干系数唯一确定，从而在非零色散下建立Hodge普适性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Riemann层次在广义标准形式下的标量τ-对称哈密顿形变，探讨其唯一性刻画，并借助Buryak--Rossi的Hodge类双分歧层次构造，推进非零色散情形下的Hodge普适性。

Method: 证明形变由其首个哈密顿密度中的系数决定：u_x^2的非零系数以及u_{xx}^g (g≥2)的系数；结合Buryak--Rossi关于Hodge类双分歧层次的构造进行论证。

Result: 标量τ-对称哈密顿形变被唯一确定，进而确定原始层次至正规Miura变换的意义下；结合已有构造得到非零色散下的Hodge普适性。

Conclusion: 该唯一性结果刻画了Riemann层次的形变，并建立非零色散处的Hodge普适性。

Abstract: We prove that a scalar tau-symmetric Hamiltonian deformation of the Riemann hierarchy in generalized standard form is uniquely determined by the nonzero coefficient of $u_x^2$ and the coefficients of $u_{xx}^g$, $g\ge2$, in its first Hamiltonian density. This determines the original hierarchy up to normal Miura transformations. Combined with the construction of Hodge-class double ramification hierarchies by Buryak--Rossi, this establishes Hodge universality at nonzero dispersion.

</details>


### [170] [Henstock--Kurzweil Gauge Integral in the Non--Gaussian Regime: A Machine--Verified Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10793)
*Yuri N. Berdinsky*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文利用Henstock–Kurzweil规范积分与Chernoff乘积逼近，在Lean 4中机器验证地构造了非高斯泛函积分，证明单模积分有限、严格正、单调且对耦合常数无穷可微，导数为收敛积分而非发散级数，多模影响泛函因子化且被高斯值界住。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统非高斯泛函积分（如phi^4作用量）依赖Wick旋转、Wiener测度、zeta正则化或从虚时间解析延拓，这些方法在数学上不够直接且难以机器验证。作者希望建立一种完全直接、不依赖上述工具的构造，并在证明助手中形式化以保证严格性。

Method: 以正定矩阵A定义有限玻色模作用量S(phi)=(1/2)phi^T A phi + lambda*sum phi_i^4；采用Henstock–Kurzweil规范积分定义单模积分I(omega,j,lambda)，并用Chernoff/Lie–Trotter分裂处理自由生成元与非高斯生成元的非交换性；所有命题在Lean 4与Mathlib中形式化，文件HkNonGaussian.lean无sorry，仅用propext、Classical.choice、Quot.sound公理。

Result: 证明单模积分有限、严格正、在[0,∞)上对lambda单调且无穷可微，其导数由phi^{4k}的收敛积分给出而非发散微扰级数；M模影响泛函因子化为单模积分之积并被其高斯值界住；给出Duffing振子、金融CEV局部波动率、Wilson–Cowan神经场、非高斯量子库四个应用示例。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种不依赖Wick旋转、Wiener测度、zeta正则化或解析延拓的非高斯泛函积分直接构造，并完成了机器验证，为数学物理与量化金融等领域的非高斯路径积分提供了严格基础。

Abstract: We develop a machine-checked construction of non-Gaussian functional integrals using the Henstock--Kurzweil gauge integral and Chernoff product approximations. The central object is a finite family of bosonic modes with action S(phi) = (1/2) phi^T A phi + lambda * sum_i phi_i^4, where A is positive definite. We prove that the one-mode integral I(omega, j, lambda) is finite, strictly positive, monotone and infinitely differentiable in the coupling lambda on [0, infinity). Its derivatives are given by convergent integrals of phi^{4k} with the same weight, not by the divergent perturbative series. The M-mode influence functional factorises into one-mode integrals and is bounded by its Gaussian value. A Chernoff / Lie--Trotter splitting handles the non-commutativity of the free and non-Gaussian generators. All statements are formalised in Lean 4 with Mathlib; the accompanying file HkNonGaussian.lean is free of sorry and uses only the standard axioms propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound. Four illustrative applications are worked out at the level of explicit formulas: the Duffing oscillator, local volatility (CEV) in finance, Wilson--Cowan neural fields, and non-Gaussian quantum reservoirs. The construction is completely direct and does not use Wick rotation, Wiener measure, zeta-regularisation or analytic continuation back from imaginary time.

</details>


### [171] [The kinematic structures and the inertial geometry of a moving charge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11165)
*César S. López-Monsalvo*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨带电粒子的运动几何在多大程度上由运动本身决定、粒子又必须"自带"什么。作者仅要求辛结构像哈密顿方程那样把速度与动量联系起来，并同时对所有能量提出这一要求，证明满足该要求的结构恰是典范结构及其被底空间闭二形式"扭曲"后的结构：场提供二形式，粒子提供其前的乘子（即电荷）。进而研究动量映射的度，决定场进入牛顿第二定律的次数：反对称双线性形式不产生洛伦兹力，而Randers度量产生洛伦兹力。作者发现该度量已内含于扭曲结构中，其输运律非线性；在不定号情形下长度与动力学分离，极值线由最短变为最长。圆球上的磁单极通量不留下此类度量，但输运仍存，预量子化将电荷限制为格点。结论：每个荷质比都有各自的几何，按功能主义标准，没有一个是带电粒子的时空。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者试图回答一个基础性问题：带电粒子运动所在的几何，有多少由运动本身固定、有多少必须由粒子"自带"。传统做法预先假定一个固定的时空几何，而本文反过来，只对辛结构提出最低要求——它需像哈密顿方程那样联系速度与动量，且对每个能量同时成立，从而探究哪些几何结构与带电粒子动力学相容，以及荷质比在几何选择中的角色。

Method: 方法上，作者对辛结构仅施加"速度-动量关系如哈密顿方程"这一约束，并要求其对所有能量成立；由此刻画满足条件的所有结构，得到典范结构及底空间闭二形式的扭曲。随后分析动量映射的度，用以判定外场进入牛顿第二定律的阶数；比较反对称双线性形式与Randers度量的行为。并借助自由能水平集上的原语、输运律、不定号签名的极值问题，以及圆球上磁单极通量与预量子化条件进行论证。

Result: 满足条件的结构恰为典范辛结构及其被底空间闭二形式的扭曲；场提供该二形式，粒子提供其乘子即电荷。单一能量支配一族结构，每个粒子采用由其电荷固定的那一个。动量映射的度决定场进入牛顿第二定律的阶数：反对称双线性形式不产生洛伦兹力，而Randers度量产生洛伦兹力（其与黎曼度量之差为速度的线性项）。该度量已内含于扭曲结构中，是其自由能水平集上的原语，且其输运律非线性。在不定号签名下，长度部分与动力学分离，极值线由最短变为最长。在圆球上，磁单极通量不留下此类度量，但输运仍存在，给予单位作用量后预量子化将电荷限制为格点。

Conclusion: 每个荷质比都对应各自的几何，因此按功能主义标准，没有任何一个可被视为带电粒子的时空。几何在很大程度上由粒子必须自带的内容（电荷、荷质比）决定，而非由运动单独固定。

Abstract: We ask how much of the geometry a charged particle moves in is fixed by its motion, and how much a particle must bring. We ask of a symplectic structure only that it relate velocity to momentum as Hamilton's equations do, and we ask it of every energy at once. In particular, we show that the structures meeting that demand are the canonical one and its twists by a closed two-form of the base. A field provides the two-form, a particle the multiplier before it, which we identify constitutively with its charge. Thus, a single energy governs a family of structures, and each particle takes the one its charge fixes. We then ask what a particle must bring to be given a momentum, and we show that the degree of that map settles the degree at which a field enters Newton's Second Law. An antisymmetric bilinear form returns no Lorentz force, whilst a Randers metric returns one --- a length whose difference from a Riemannian one is linear in the velocity. Moreover, we find that metric already within the twisted structure, as its primitive over a level of the free energy, and its law of transport to be nonlinear, no affine connection being known to serve. Under an indefinite signature the length parts from the dynamics, and the extremals turn from shortest to longest. On the round sphere a monopole flux leaves no such metric, whilst the transport remains and prequantisation, given a unit of action, restricts the charge to a lattice. In this manner, we conclude that each charge-to-mass ratio receives a geometry of its own, so that by a functionalist criterion none of them is the spacetime of a charged particle.

</details>


### [172] [A Time-Frequency Framework for GKP Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10802)
*Franz Luef,Eduard Ortega*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文构建格 GKP 码的时频框架：理想码字实现于调制空间 M^∞，经向量值 Zak 变换等同于连续症状环面上的有限逻辑纤维；多窗 Gabor 分析将逻辑向量表示为有限伴随格系数块。证明归一化块映射为等距、给出显式恢复投影与稳定逻辑重构，构造格包络 Gabor 乘子型可归一化 GKP 逼近并建立弱-* 收敛与渐近等距编码，最后从平移系数块的相位关系恢复位移症状并刻画其扰动稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为格 GKP 码建立严格的时频分析基础：将连续变量量子纠错中的理想码字与可实现的编码/恢复算子纳入统一的 Gabor 框架，并解决实际实现中的可归一化逼近、稳定重构与症状提取问题。

Method: 采用调制空间 M^∞ 中的理想码字、向量值 Zak 变换、多窗 Gabor 分析、伴随格系数块、格包络 Gabor 乘子、弱-* 收敛与相位关系分析。

Result: 归一化块映射为等距；获得显式恢复投影与稳定逻辑重构；构造可归一化 GKP 逼近并证明弱-* 收敛与渐近等距编码；由平移系数块相位关系恢复位移症状并量化加性扰动下的稳定性。

Conclusion: 该框架将格 GKP 码的逻辑编码、重构与症状读取统一表述为调制空间/Gabor 分析问题，为连续变量量子纠错提供可实现的稳定逼近与误差分析基础。

Abstract: We develop a time--frequency framework for lattice GKP codes in which ideal codewords are realized in the modulation space $M^\infty$ and identified, through a vector-valued Zak transform, with a finite logical fibre over the continuous syndrome torus. Multi-window Gabor analysis then represents the logical vector by a finite block of adjoint-lattice coefficients. We prove that the normalized block map is an isometry, obtain an explicit recovering projection, and derive stable logical reconstruction. We further construct normalizable GKP approximants as lattice-envelope Gabor multipliers and establish weak-$*$ convergence and asymptotically isometric encoding. Finally, we recover displacement syndromes from phase relations between translated coefficient blocks and quantify their stability under additive perturbations.

</details>


### [173] [Probabilistic representation and limit theorems for particle numbers of quasi-free states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10840)
*Fanch Coudreuse,Simone Rademacher,Oliver Tse*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 研究了局部相互作用玻色准自由态的粒子数分布，证明其可分解为独立几何随机变量之和，并给出了尾部界、小数目律、大偏差原理及中心极限定理，应用于玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚的量子损耗统计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局部相互作用玻色准自由态在数学物理多个领域中出现，其粒子数分布是物理文献中的预测对象，但从数学上严格刻画其统计规律尚缺乏系统分析。

Method: 通过将粒子数表示为无穷多个独立几何分布随机变量之和，对这一表示进行严格证明，并在此基础上推导指数尾部界、小数目律、对数速率大偏差原理和中心极限定理。

Result: 得到粒子数的指数尾部界；当格点间距发散时给出小数目律与对数速率大偏差原理；当格点间距趋于零时给出中心极限定理。并将结果应用于玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚中量子损耗的统计描述，其中常数可由相互作用势的散射长度显式给出。

Conclusion: 该工作验证了物理文献中的预测，为局部相互作用玻色准自由态的粒子数统计提供了严格的数学框架，并深化了对量子损耗统计的理解。

Abstract: We study the particle number distribution of locally interacting bosonic quasi- free states, which arise in various areas of mathematical physics. We show that the particle number decomposes into an infinite sum of independent geometrically distributed random variables, confirming predictions from the physics literature. This representation yields exponential tail bounds for the particle number, a law of small numbers together with a large deviation principle at logarithmic speed when the lattice spacing diverges, and a central limit theorem when it vanishes. As an application, we obtain a detailed description of the statistics of the quantum depletion in Bose--Einstein condensates, with the constants explicit in terms of the scattering length of the interaction potential.

</details>


### [174] [Why we should condition denoising diffusion generative models on windows of past observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10924)
*Matthias Morzfeld,Daniel Hodyss*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文将扩散生成模型的条件设定在过去观测的短时间窗口上，提出扩散DA系统与扩散DOP系统两种训练框架，从而在不重新训练的情况下逼近完全循环DA/DOP系统的最小后验误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数据同化（DA）是依赖时间相关先验的循环过程，将过去观测信息传播到未来循环。而将去噪扩散生成模型用于DA面临困难：标准方法使用固定训练集，导致先验静态化，忽略过去观测信息，后验误差大于循环DA系统；若引入时间相关先验，则需频繁且昂贵的重新训练。因此需要一种无需重新训练即可利用过去观测的生成式DA方案。

Method: 受线性系统理论启发——卡尔曼滤波预测对过去观测的依赖呈指数衰减——作者将扩散模型条件化于过去观测的短时间窗口，并提出两套训练框架：一是扩散DA系统，在给定一组过去观测下预测当前状态；二是扩散“直接观测预测”（DOP）系统，在给定过去观测下预测未来观测。采用典型线性系统进行验证，以保证解析可处理性并排除神经网络训练误差的干扰。

Result: 在典型线性系统上，两个系统均能达成完全循环DA/DOP系统所具备的最小后验误差，且无需重新训练，前提是时间窗口足够长。线性设置确保了解析可追踪性，并确认以过去观测窗口为条件是高效且准确的扩散DA/DOP所必需的。

Conclusion: 条件化于过去观测的短窗口可使扩散DA与扩散DOP在不重新训练的情况下逼近循环系统的最小后验误差，说明窗口条件化是高效准确扩散DA/DOP的必要条件。

Abstract: Data assimilation (DA) is, traditionally, a cycling process that relies on time-dependent priors to propagate information from past observations to future cycles. Using denoising diffusion generative modeling for DA is challenging because standard approaches use a fixed training data set, which in turn leads to a static prior that ignores information from past observations. Because past observations are ignored, DA systems with static priors lead to larger posterior errors than cycling DA systems. Incorporating time-dependent priors into generative models, however, requires expensive and frequent retraining. Motivated by linear systems theory - where the dependence of a prediction of a Kalman filter on past observations decays exponentially - we condition diffusion models on short windows of past observations. Specifically, we describe training procedures for two frameworks: a diffusion DA system predicting the current state given a set of past observations, and a diffusion ``direct observation prediction'' (DOP) system, predicting future observations given a set of past observations. Using a canonical linear system, we show that both systems can achieve the minimal posterior error characteristic of a fully-cycled DA/DOP system, without re-training, provided the time windows are long enough. The linear setup ensures analytical tractability, avoids confounding neural network training errors, and confirms that conditioning on windows of past observations is required for efficient and accurate diffusion-based DA or DOP.

</details>


### [175] [Recent Advances on a Model for the Electromagnetic Dirac Field at Thermal Equilibrium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11283)
*Umberto Morellini*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 综述了正温度下相对论电子-正电子场与经典电磁场相互作用模型的近期进展，旨在理解热背景对量子系统极化机制的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解热背景（正温度环境）如何影响相对论量子系统的极化机制，这一问题在现有研究中尚不清晰，故需推进该模型的认识。

Method: 采用相对论电子-正电子场与经典电磁场相互作用的模型，在正温度（有限温度）条件下进行理论分析，并综述近期相关进展。

Result: （摘要为综述性质，未给出具体数值或实验结论）汇总了该模型在有限温度下的近期理论进展。

Conclusion: 该综述为理解热背景对相对论电子-正电子系统极化机制的作用提供了基础，最终目标是深化对这一量子系统极化行为的认识。

Abstract: We review recent progress on a model for the relativistic electron-positron field interacting with a classical electromagnetic field at positive temperature. The ultimate goal is to advance our comprehension of how thermal backgrounds affect polarisation mechanisms of this quantum system.

</details>


### [176] [On asymptotic expansions of the density of states for Poisson distributed random Schrödinger operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11603)
*Jan Fischer,David Hasler,Jannis Koberstein*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 研究Poisson过程势的随机Schrödinger算子，在小无序极限下推导预解式迹的渐近展开，给出展开系数的显式估计并证明其无穷体积极限在谱参数趋近自由Laplacian谱时有限，应用于积分态密度界的推导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机Schrödinger算子的小无序行为是数学物理中的重要问题，其中Poisson型随机势的预解式迹的渐近展开及其系数的体积极限性质尚待系统刻画。

Method: 对具有Poisson过程分布势的随机Schrödinger算子，在小无序极限下建立预解式迹的渐近展开，并对展开系数进行显式估计，进一步分析其无穷体积极限。

Result: 得到预解式迹的渐近展开；给出展开系数的显式估计；证明当谱参数趋近自由Laplacian的谱时，系数的无穷体积极限有限。

Conclusion: 作为应用，基于上述展开与系数性质推导出积分态密度的界。

Abstract: We study a random Schrödinger operator with a potential distributed according to a Poisson process. Asymptotic expansions for traces of resolvents in the limit of small disorder are derived. Explicit estimates for the expansion coefficients are given and we show that their infinite volume limits are finite as the spectral parameter approaches the spectrum of the free Laplacian. As an application we derive bounds on the integrated density of states.

</details>


### [177] [A closed signature formula for the Katz-Long-Moody Hermitian form](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11793)
*Haru Negami*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 对Katz-Long-Moody构造，作者给出了不变Hermitian形式签名的闭式公式，据此判定其正定性（definiteness），并应用于Pochhammer系统、Beukers-Heckman交错准则及Hecke与Temperley-Lieb特化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Katz-Long-Moody构造在纯辫群上对应Haraoka的乘性中值卷积；在酉输入与单位圆上非1的卷参数下，商表示带有规范的非退化不变Hermitian形式，但其签名与正定性问题此前未解决（见companion paper提出的definiteness问题）。

Method: 初等线性代数：一个行列式恒等式、显式的分块主元（block-pivot）公式，以及关于酉矩阵Cayley变换之和的惯性（inertia）公式。签名公式以输入局部单值性的特征角、其有序积的特征角以及卷参数表示，并计入到商之前的核（kernel）以及共振参数处的签名变化。

Result: 给出签名闭式公式；精确判定诱导形式何时为正定，从而解决companion paper的正定性问题。正定蕴含输出表示可酉化；当输出表示不可约时，逆命题成立。

Conclusion: 应用包括：显式比较秩一构造与Haraoka对Pochhammer系统的不变形式；复现Beukers-Heckman交错准则的Gauss情形；确定Hecke与Temperley-Lieb特化的正定参数区间。

Abstract: The Katz-Long-Moody construction associates to a representation of the semidirect product of a free group and a braid group, defined by the Artin action, and a nonzero parameter a new representation of the same group. On the pure braid group it corresponds to Haraoka's multiplicative middle convolution for KZ-type equations. For unitary input and a convolution parameter on the unit circle other than one, the construction equips the quotient representation with a canonical non-degenerate invariant Hermitian form. We give a closed formula for its signature in terms of the eigenangles of the input local monodromies, the eigenangles of their ordered product, and the convolution parameter. The formula accounts for the kernel of the form before passage to the quotient and for signature changes at resonant parameters. It determines precisely when the induced form is definite, answering the definiteness problem posed in the companion paper. Definiteness implies unitarizability of the output representation; the converse holds when that representation is irreducible.
  The proof uses elementary linear algebra: a determinant identity, explicit block-pivot formulas, and an inertia formula for sums of Cayley transforms of unitary matrices. As applications, we compare the rank-one construction explicitly with Haraoka's invariant form for Pochhammer systems, recover the Gauss case of the Beukers-Heckman interlacing criterion, and determine the definite parameter intervals for the Hecke and Temperley-Lieb specializations.

</details>


<div id='math.AG'></div>

# math.AG [[Back]](#toc)

### [178] [Long range divided differences, clusters, and Graham-positivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10735)
*Hunter Spink,Vasu Tewari*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 研究正簇扇中锥所对应的A型完全旗簇环面轨道闭包及其左$S_n$平移，用非交叉交替森林编码的长程除差操作计算环面等变度映射，给出将环面等变同调类展开为Graham正Schubert闭链组合的算法，并推出若干Graham正展开结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 环面轨道闭包在旗簇几何与簇代数中具有重要地位，但其等变同调类在Schubert基下的正性展开（Graham正性）尚缺乏统一、组合化的算法框架。本文旨在为与正簇扇中锥关联的环面轨道闭包建立可计算的等变度映射与Graham正Schubert闭链展开方法。

Method: 研究对象为A型完全旗簇中正簇扇内锥自然对应的环面轨道闭包及其左$S_n$平移。方法上，将环面等变度映射表示为以非交叉交替森林编码的长程除差算子的复合，并设计组合算法把环面等变同调类展开为Graham正的Schubert闭链线性组合。随后投影到Grassmann簇以获得相应的Grassmann Schubert闭链分解。

Result: 得到所有环面不变曲线的组合Graham正Schubert闭链展开（推广了环面不动点的AJS-Billey公式）、一般环面轨道闭包的展开，以及Bruhat区间$[w,wc']$（$c'\le s_{n-1}s_{n-2}\cdots s_1$）上Richardson簇的展开。投影到Grassmann簇后，还得到与置换基集上的格路拟阵关联的环面轨道闭包的Graham正Grassmann Schubert闭链分解。

Conclusion: 本文建立了以非交叉交替森林与长程除差操作为核心的组合框架，统一给出与正簇扇中锥关联的环面轨道闭包（含环面不变曲线、一般轨道闭包及特定Richardson簇）的Graham正Schubert闭链展开，并将结果推广至Grassmann簇与格路拟阵情形，推广了AJS-Billey公式。

Abstract: We study torus-orbit closures in the type $A$ complete flag variety naturally associated to cones in the positive cluster fan, together with their left $S_n$-translates. The torus-equivariant degree maps can be computed via composites of long-range divided difference operations encoded by noncrossing alternating forests, and we give combinatorial algorithms to expand the torus-equivariant homology classes into Graham-positive combinations of Schubert cycles. As applications we obtain combinatorial Graham-positive Schubert cycle expansions for all torus-invariant curves (generalizing the AJS-Billey formula for torus-fixed points), generic torus-orbit closures, and Richardson varieties for Bruhat intervals $[w,wc']$ where $c'\le s_{n-1}s_{n-2}\cdots s_1$. Projecting to Grassmannians we also obtain Graham-positive Grassmannian Schubert cycle decompositions of torus-orbit closures associated to lattice path matroids on permuted ground sets.

</details>


### [179] [The Reye geometry inside the 64 lines of the Schur quartic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10751)
*Paweł Nurowski*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了Naskręcki–Pokora (24_4,32_3)构形中蕴含的经典几何：在Höhn的D_4标号下，24个根上的对径对合诱导出无不动点的商构形，恰为经典Reye构形；并确定完整64线关联几何的自同构群阶为4608，两个构形构成单一轨道，稳定子群阶为2304（射影意义下576）；64条线可典范地扩展为176线构形，由六个射影等价的Schur四次曲面承载。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于发掘Schur四次曲面上Naskręcki–Pokora构形背后隐藏的经典几何结构，建立其与经典Reye构形之间的联系，并刻画相关关联几何的对称性。

Method: 采用Höhn的D_4根标号，利用24个根上的对径对合构造无不动点商；计算完整64线关联几何的自同构群及其轨道与稳定子；并研究64条线到176线构形的典范扩展。

Result: 对径对合诱导的商恰为经典Reye构形；64线关联几何的自同构群阶为4608，两个Naskręcki–Pokora构形构成单一轨道，稳定子阶为2304（射影下576）；64条线典范扩展为176线构形，满足176=16+16+9·16，由六个射影等价的Schur四次曲面承载，诱导曲面置换群为S_3×S_3。

Conclusion: 该研究将Schur四次曲面上的现代构形与经典Reye构形联系起来，并完整刻画了相关64线及176线关联几何的对称结构与扩展形式。

Abstract: We identify the classical geometry hidden in the Naskręcki--Pokora $(24_4,32_3)$ configuration on the Schur quartic. In Höhn's $D_4$ labelling, the antipodal involution on the $24$ roots induces a fixed-point-free quotient of the incidence configuration, and this quotient is precisely the classical Reye configuration. We also determine the symmetry of the complete $64$-line incidence geometry: its automorphism group has order $4608$, the two Naskręcki--Pokora configurations form a single orbit, and the stabilizer of either has order $2304$ (projectively, $576$). Finally, the $64$ lines extend canonically to a $176$-line arrangement carried by six projectively equivalent Schur quartics, with $176=16+16+9\cdot16$ and induced surface permutation group $S_3\times S_3$.

</details>


### [180] [Torelli theorem for the product of moduli spaces of vector bundles over a curve](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10764)
*Indranil Biswas,Arijit Dey,Anubhab Pahari*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明了光滑射影曲线（亏格 g≥4）上半稳定向量丛模空间乘积的 Torelli 型定理，并给出相应模栈乘积的类似结果，进而计算其自同构群。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模空间通常决定其底曲线（Torelli 型问题），但多个模空间的乘积是否能决定各因子的曲线与丛结构仍需研究；本文旨在将 Torelli 型结论推广到乘积情形，并借此确定自同构群。

Method: 利用关于 Picard 秩为一且 Picard 群离散的正规射影簇乘积的分解定理，刻画半稳定向量丛模空间的乘积，并平行处理模栈情形。

Result: 建立了半稳定向量丛模空间乘积（g≥4，光滑射影曲线）的 Torelli 型定理，并证明模栈乘积的类似结果；同时得到该乘积分解定理。

Conclusion: 作为应用，计算了这些模空间乘积与模栈乘积的自同构群。

Abstract: We prove a Torelli-type theorem for a product of the moduli spaces of semistable vector bundles over smooth projective curves of genus $g\,\ge\, 4$. A similar result is proved for the product of the moduli stacks of semistable vector bundles. This is proved using a decomposition theorem for the product of the normal projective varieties with Picard rank one and discrete Picard group. As an application, we compute the automorphism group of the product of the moduli spaces and moduli stacks.

</details>


### [181] [Perverse Euler Characteristics of Hermitian Locally Symmetric Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10946)
*Mert Akdenizli,Quoc-Anh Tran*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 证明了有限体积非紧型局部Hermite对称空间的非负反常Euler示性数；通过对光滑环面紧化的对数余切丛建立nef性，并结合反常层Euler示性数的正性判别法得到该结论，且对满支撑反常层不等式严格成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究有限体积非紧型局部Hermite对称空间上反常Euler示性数的符号性质，并推广到各类模空间。

Method: 证明光滑环面紧化的对数余切丛的nef性，结合反常层Euler示性数的正性判别准则。

Result: 有限体积非紧型局部Hermite对称空间的反常Euler示性数非负；对满支撑的反常层不等式严格成立；并在多种模空间上得到相应的非负性结果。

Conclusion: 对数余切丛的nef性可用于推导反常Euler示性数的非负性及严格性，并被应用于模空间。

Abstract: We prove that finite-volume locally Hermitian symmetric spaces of noncompact type have nonnegative perverse Euler characteristics. To show this, we obtain a nefness result for the logarithmic cotangent bundle of a smooth toroidal compactification. Combining this with a positivity criterion for Euler characteristics of perverse sheaves, we deduce the nonnegativity result. We further prove that the inequality is strict for perverse sheaves with full support. As applications, we get nonnegativity results for perverse Euler characteristics on various moduli spaces.

</details>


### [182] [Coupled Pklt Tuples and Varieties of Pklt Type](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11107)
*Donghyeon Kim,Dae-Won Lee*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文引入渐近乘子理想层与对数典范阈值（针对投射klt对上拟有效除子组），并研究其与拟单项赋值、Zariski分解、多截面环有限生成及Mori梦空间判据的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在极小模型纲领框架下，将乘子理想层与对数典范阈值推广到拟有效除子组（元组）情形，并刻画potential klt型簇的几何性质，为Mori梦空间提供判定工具。

Method: 定义与拟有效除子组相联系的渐近乘子理想层与对数典范阈值；利用拟单项赋值计算coupled potentially klt元组的阈值；对potential klt型簇构造大除子的双有理Zariski分解（其正部为半丰沛）。

Result: 证明coupled potentially klt元组的阈值由拟单项赋值计算；证明potential klt型簇上任意大除子存在正部半丰沛的双有理Zariski分解；证明大除子多截面环的有限生成性；给出potential klt型簇为Mori梦空间的判据。

Conclusion: 该工作将渐近乘子理想层与对数典范阈值理论推广至拟有效除子组，并揭示了potential klt型簇上Zariski分解、多截面环有限生成与Mori梦空间之间的紧密联系。

Abstract: We introduce asymptotic multiplier ideal sheaves and log canonical thresholds associated with tuples of pseudoeffective divisors on a projective klt pair. We prove that the threshold of a coupled potentially klt tuple is computed by a quasi-monomial valuation. For varieties of potentially klt type, we prove that every big divisor admits a birational Zariski decomposition with semiample positive part. We also prove finite generation of multisection rings of big divisors and give a criterion for a variety of potentially klt type to be a Mori dream space.

</details>


### [183] [Graded Betti numbers of general curves of large degree](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11161)
*JeongDon Lee,Li Li,Jinhyung Park*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 该论文给出了曲线C上除子L的Koszul上同调与分次Betti数的显式闭式公式，并在一般曲线情形下确定完整Betti表，同时计算Boij–Söderberg系数与渐近纯性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管Koszul上同调群K_{p,q}(C,L)的消失与非消失在大次数L下已知，但分次Betti数κ_{p,q}(C,L)的具体数值仍大多未知，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 在W_k^1(C)具有预期维数且H^1(C,L⊗ω_C^{-1})=0的条件下，推导所有分次Betti数的显式闭式公式；并分析截面环R(C,L)的Boij–Söderberg系数。

Result: 得到全部κ_{p,q}(C,L)的闭式公式；确定当deg L≥4g-3，或deg L≥3g-3且L一般时一般曲线的完整Betti表；显式计算控制渐近纯性的Boij–Söderberg系数；证明其余系数在超椭圆曲线下递减，在相关Brill–Noether轨迹的自然一般约化性假设下递增（最终单调性）。

Conclusion: 该工作将曲线Koszul上同调的研究从消失性推进到Betti数精确计算，并刻画了截面环的Boij–Söderberg系数与渐近纯性及单调性行为。

Abstract: Let $C$ be a smooth projective complex curve of genus $g$ and gonality $k$, and $L$ be a very ample line bundle on $C$. When $L$ has sufficiently large degree, the vanishing and nonvanishing of the Koszul cohomology groups $K_{p,q}(C,L)$ have been determined previously, but the exact values of the graded Betti numbers $κ_{p,q}(C, L)$ remain largely unknown. In this paper, we give explicit closed formulas for all graded Betti numbers $κ_{p,q}(C, L)$ when the Brill--Noether locus $W_k^1(C)$ has the expected dimension and $H^1(C, L \otimes ω_C^{-1})=0$. Consequently, we determine the complete Betti table for a general curve when $°L \geq 4g-3$ or when $°L \geq 3g-3$ and $L$ is general. We also explicitly compute the Boij--Söderberg coefficient of the section ring $R(C, L)$ governing asymptotic purity, and show eventual monotonicity of the remaining coefficients: they decrease for hyperelliptic curves and increase under a natural generic reducedness assumption on the relevant Brill--Noether loci.

</details>


### [184] [Divisibility and torsion in higher Chow groups over arithmetic fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11178)
*Toshiro Hiranouchi,Rin Sugiyama*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 研究光滑概形上高阶Chow群 $CH^{d+i}(X,j)$ 的Abel群结构，证明在 $i$ 大于等于域 $F$ 的 $l$-上同调维数时的可除性与无挠性，并研究推前映射的核，应用于有限域、局部域和整体域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刻画高阶Chow群 $CH^{d+i}(X,j)$ 作为Abel群的结构（可除性、挠性），这类群在代数K理论与代数簇的代数环理论中具有核心地位，但其群结构在一般情形下难以计算，因此需要在特定条件下（大 $i$ 相对余维数）建立结构性结论。

Method: 设 $X$ 为域 $F$ 上维数 $d$ 的光滑概形；取素数 $l \neq \mathrm{char}(F)$，在 $i$ 大于等于 $F$ 的 $l$-上同调维数时，通过 $l$-进方法（上同调工具）分析 $CH^{d+i}(X,j)$ 的可除性与无挠性。对光滑、紧合且几何不可约的 $X$，进一步研究推前映射 $CH^{d+i}(X,j)\to CH^i(F,j)$ 的核。

Result: 对 $l \neq \mathrm{char}(F)$ 且 $i \ge l\text{-cd}(F)$，得到 $CH^{d+i}(X,j)$ 的（$l$-）可除性与无挠性结果；对光滑紧合几何不可约的 $X$，刻画了 $CH^{d+i}(X,j)\to CH^i(F,j)$ 的核。并把这些结果用于有限域、局部域与整体域。

Conclusion: 在相对余维数较大的情形下，高阶Chow群 $CH^{d+i}(X,j)$ 呈现出良好的Abel群结构（可除、无挠），且推前映射的核可被控制；这些结论统一适用于有限域、局部域与整体域等经典数域情形。

Abstract: Let $X$ be a smooth scheme of dimension $d$ over a field $F$. We study the abelian-group structure of the higher Chow groups $CH^{d+i}(X,j)$. For a prime $l$ different from the characteristic of $F$, we prove divisibility and torsion-freeness results when $i\ge$ the $l$-cohomological dimension of $F$. If $X$ is smooth proper and geometrically irreducible, we also study the kernel of the push-forward $CH^{d+i}(X,j)\to CH^i(F,j)$. We apply these results to finite fields, local fields, and global fields.

</details>


### [185] [Openness of local properties in flat families of superschemes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11287)
*Ibrahim Ahmad*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 该文证明了超概形的平展真态射的纤维在正规性、GFRR、正则性与Cohen–Macaulay性上构成开集，从而实现特殊纤维到一般纤维的性质提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Jankowski 借助 Serre 判据定义了超概形的正规性与约化性（即 generic fermionic regularity 与 reducedness）。作者希望将这些性质在态射族中的开性（openness）与形变/退化（degeneration）提升问题推广到超几何情形。

Method: 设 f: X → Y 为平展（flat）、真（proper）态射，X 为超概形，Y 为特征 0 域上有限型的 Dedekind 概形。作者运用 Serre 判据的超版本研究纤维 X_y 的正规性、GFRR、正则性和 Cohen–Macaulay 性，并分析这些性质在基 Y 上的开性。

Result: 证明了对上述态射，使纤维 X_y 正规、GFRR、正则且 Cohen–Macaulay 的 y ∈ Y 的集合在 Y 中是开集。

Conclusion: 特别地，对于 A_C^{1|0} 上的退化，这些性质可由特殊纤维提升到一般纤维，给出了超概形版本的形变/退化性质提升结论。

Abstract: Recently, Jankowski introduced the Serre criteria for superschemes to define the super versions of normality and reducedness (called generic fermionic regularity and reducedness). We show that for a flat, proper morphism $f:X\rightarrow Y$, where $X$ is a superscheme and $Y$ is a Dedekind scheme of finite type over a field of characteristic $0$, the set of $y\in Y$ for which the fibers $X_y$ are normal, GFRR, regular, and Cohen-Macaulay is open in $Y$. In particular, if one has a degeneration over $\mathbb{A}_{\mathbb{C}}^{1|0}$, this shows that these properties can be lifted from the special fiber to the generic one.

</details>


### [186] [Generic vanishing conjecture for divisors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11377)
*Fanjun Meng*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文受Pareschi-Popa一般性消没猜想的启发，证明了该猜想在余维1情形下的类似结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Pareschi与Popa提出的一般性消没猜想，关注主极化阿贝尔簇上余维数大于1、表示极小上同调类的子簇的存在性，与其理想层经主极化扭曲后的正性之间的关系。作者受此表述启发，希望探讨该关系在余维1情形下的对应版本。

Method: 采用代数几何方法，类比Pareschi-Popa一般性消没猜想的表述框架，将研究对象限定于余维1的子簇情形，建立相应的猜想类比并进行证明。

Result: 证明了Pareschi-Popa一般性消没猜想的余维1类比（codimension 1 analog）。

Conclusion: 主极化阿贝尔簇上子簇的极小上同调类表示与理想层扭曲正性之间的关系，在余维1情形下存在与一般性消没猜想相平行的结论。

Abstract: Generic vanishing conjecture proposed by Pareschi and Popa is about the relationship between the existence of subvarieties of codimension $>1$ of principally polarized abelian varieties representing minimal cohomology class and the positivity of their ideal sheaves twisted by the principal polarization. Inspired by their formulation, we prove a codimension $1$ analog of generic vanishing conjecture.

</details>


### [187] [Campana Rational Connectedness and Weak Approximation of Del Pezzo Orbifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11533)
*Saptarshi Dandapat*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 证明次数小于7的del Pezzo orbifold及边界不可约的高次数del Pezzo orbifold是强Campana单有理的，进而推出Campana有理连通，给出Campana猜想在二维的重要非环面例子并导出Campana纤维的弱逼近。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为Campana猜想提供二维非环面（non-toric）例子，并以弱逼近定理所需的形式给出结果，从而将结论应用于Campana纤维的Campana截面的弱逼近问题。

Method: 对del Pezzo orbifold按次数与边界不可约性分类讨论：次数小于7的情形与高次数且边界不可约的情形分别处理，证明其强Campana单有理性；附录中分类次数为6、7、8且边界不可约的del Pezzo orbifold。

Result: 所考虑的del Pezzo orbifold被证明是强Campana单有理的，并由此推得Campana有理连通；同时完成次数6、7、8且边界不可约的del Pezzo orbifold的分类。

Conclusion: 该工作验证了Campana猜想在二维的一类非环面情形，并给出一般纤维为上述orbifold的Campana纤维其Campana截面在所有位置的弱逼近性质。

Abstract: We prove that a del Pezzo orbifold of degree less than 7, and del Pezzo orbifolds of higher degree with irreducible boundary are strongly Campana uniruled, and deduce that it is Campana rationally connected. This provides important non-toric examples in dimension two of a conjecture by Campana, in the form required by the weak approximation theorem, and yields weak approximation for Campana sections of Campana fibrations at all places whose general fibre is such an orbifold. In three appendices we classify the del Pezzo orbifolds of degree 6, 7 and 8 with irreducible boundary.

</details>


### [188] [A maximal function field of genus $17$ over $\mathbb{F}_{11^2}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11679)
*Peter Beelen,Maria Montanucci,Jonathan Niemann*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文构造了有限域 $\mathbb{F}_{11^2}$ 上一个新的极大函数域 $G$，证明它不能作为 Hermitian 函数域的子域得到，这是首个满足该性质的 $\mathbb{F}_{p^2}$-极大函数域，并计算了 $G$ 的自同构群。该极大函数域借助 AI 发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典极大函数域多来自 Hermitian 函数域及其子域，本文旨在寻找不属于这一来源的新极大函数域，以拓宽对 $\mathbb{F}_{p^2}$-极大函数域结构（特别是非 Hermitian 子域型）的认识。

Method: 在有限域 $\mathbb{F}_{11^2}$ 上构造一个新的极大函数域 $G$；通过代数/算术论证证明 $G$ 不是 Hermitian 函数域的子域；并计算其自同构群。该域的发现使用了 AI 方法。

Result: 得到一个新的 $\mathbb{F}_{11^2}$-极大函数域 $G$，证明其不能作为 Hermitian 函数域的子域，成为首个具此性质的 $\mathbb{F}_{p^2}$-极大函数域；同时给出了 $G$ 的自同构群。

Conclusion: 存在不属于 Hermitian 函数域子域类的 $\mathbb{F}_{p^2}$-极大函数域，扩展了对极大函数域分类与构造的理解；AI 可辅助发现此类新域。

Abstract: In this article, we describe a new maximal function field $G$ over the finite field $\mathbb{F}_{11^2}$ with $11^2$ elements and show that it cannot be obtained as a subfield of the Hermitian function field. This provides the first known $\mathbb{F}_{p^2}$-maximal function field that satisfies this property. Further, we compute the automorphism group of $G$. The new maximal function field was found using AI.

</details>


### [189] [Maximality of the Hodge level for smooth weighted complete intersections of general type](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11825)
*Victor Przyjalkowski*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 证明了一般型光滑良构加权完全交的Hodge数 $h^{0,n}(X)$ 为正（即Hodge水平极大），并给出了几何亏格 $p_g(X)$ 的显式下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究一般型光滑良构加权完全交的Hodge水平是否极大，并为几何亏格提供定量下界。

Method: 针对光滑良构的一般型加权完全交进行Hodge数与几何亏格的分析与估计。

Result: 对任意维数 $n>0$ 的光滑良构一般型加权完全交，$h^{0,n}(X)>0$，Hodge水平极大；并得到 $p_g(X)$ 的显式下界。以此推出唯一非（具有 $p_g=1$ 的线性锥的）数值相交的一般型加权完全交是 $X_{6,6}\subset\mathbb{P}(1,2,2,3,3)$。

Conclusion: 一般型加权完全交的Hodge水平极大，几何亏格有显式下界，且例外情形被完全刻画。

Abstract: We prove that for every smooth well formed weighted complete intersection of general type of dimension $n>0$, the Hodge number $h^{0,n}(X)$ is positive; in other words, its Hodge level is maximal. We also obtain an explicit lower bound for the geometric genus $p_g(X)$. This implies that the only weighted complete intersection of general type that is not a numerical intersection with a linear cone with $p_g(X)=1$ is $X_{6,6}\subset\mathbb{P}(1,2,2,3,3)$.

</details>


<div id='math.DG'></div>

# math.DG [[Back]](#toc)

### [190] [Twistor theory of Loxodromes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10761)
*Maciej Dunajski,Wojciech Kryński*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 利用twistor对应，将 $S^4$ 中loxodrome刻画为 $\mathbb{CP}^3$ 中一族14维齐次直纹曲面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: twistor对应给出 $S^4$ 中曲线与 $\mathbb{CP}^3$ 中直纹曲面的对偶，前者的共形性质反映为后者的射影性质；本文旨在用这一对偶来刻画与 $S^4$ 中loxodrome相对应的直纹曲面族。

Method: 以twistor对应（twistor correspondence）为工具，将 $S^4$ 中曲线的共形几何性质转化为 $\mathbb{CP}^3$ 中直纹曲面的射影性质，并对相应的齐次直纹曲面族进行分类刻画。

Result: 刻画了 $\mathbb{CP}^3$ 中一个14维的齐次直纹曲面族，该族恰好对应于 $S^4$ 中的loxodrome。

Conclusion: twistor对偶被成功用于将 $S^4$ 中loxodrome的共形刻画转译为 $\mathbb{CP}^3$ 中直纹曲面的射影刻画，得到14维齐次直纹曲面族这一对应结果。

Abstract: The twistor correspondence provides a duality between curves in $S^4$ and ruled surfaces in $\mathbb{CP}^3$ in which the conformal properties of the former are reflected in the projective properties of the latter. We use this to characterise a $14$--dimensional family of homogeneous ruled surfaces in $\mathbb{CP}^3$ which correspond to loxodromes in $S^4$.

</details>


### [191] [The $q$-deformed cross-ratio: modular invariants and Coxeter friezes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10999)
*Valentin Ovsienko*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文引入并研究 $\mathbb P^1(\mathbb Q)$ 上 cross-ratio 的标量 $q$-形变，构造基于 Morier-Genoud 与作者的 $q$-形变有理数概念。该 $q$-cross-ratio 在 $\mathrm{PSL}(2,\mathbb{Z})$ 下不变，而行列式为 $-1$ 的 $\mathrm{PGL}(2,\mathbb{Z})$ 元素按 $q\mapsto q^{-1}$ 作用。主要结果包括它与有理多边形对应的 $q$-形变 Coxeter friezes 的联系，以及在 $q=e^h$ 展开下得到一列代数无关的模不变量与相对不变量（尽管该序列不分离模轨道），并显式计算了展开的前两个非常数系数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典 cross-ratio 是射影几何与模群作用下的基本不变量。作者希望借助 $q$-形变有理数框架，构造 cross-ratio 的量子/$q$-形变版本，以统一理解模不变量、Coxeter friezes 与有理多边形之间的结构联系，并探索 $q$-展开产生的模不变量序列及其轨道分离能力。

Method: 基于 Morier-Genoud 与作者的 $q$-形变有理数定义，构造 $\mathbb P^1(\mathbb Q)$ 上 cross-ratio 的标量 $q$-形变，即 $q$-cross-ratio；分析其在 $\mathrm{PSL}(2,\mathbb{Z})$ 与 $\mathrm{PGL}(2,\mathbb{Z})$ 下的变换性质；建立其与有理多边形相关 $q$-形变 Coxeter friezes 的关系；对 $q=e^h$ 进行展开并计算前两个非常数系数。

Result: $q$-cross-ratio 在 $\mathrm{PSL}(2,\mathbb{Z})$ 下保持不变，$\mathrm{PGL}(2,\mathbb{Z})$ 中行列式为 $-1$ 的元素使其按 $q\mapsto q^{-1}$ 变换；它与有理多边形对应的 $q$-形变 Coxeter friezes 存在主要联系；$q=e^h$ 展开给出代数无关的模不变量与相对不变量序列，但该序列不分离模轨道；前两个非常数系数被显式算出。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了 $q$-cross-ratio 作为模群作用下不变量/相对不变量的框架，并揭示其与 $q$-形变 Coxeter friezes 的深层联系。虽然由展开得到的不变量序列代数无关且不分离模轨道，但为后续研究 $q$-形变射影几何、模不变量结构及 frieze 模式提供了新工具与具体计算基础。

Abstract: We introduce and study a scalar $q$-deformation of the cross-ratio on $\mathbb P^1(\mathbb Q)$. Our construction is based on the notion of $q$-deformed rational numbers due to Morier-Genoud and the author. The $q$-cross-ratio is invariant under $\mathrm{PSL}(2,\mathbb{Z})$, while elements of determinant $-1$ of $\mathrm{PGL}(2,\mathbb{Z})$ act by $q\mapsto q^{-1}$. A principal result is its relation to $q$-deformed Coxeter friezes associated with rational polygons. The expansion at $q=e^h$ yields an algebraically independent sequence of modular invariants and relative invariants, although this sequence does not separate modular orbits. We compute the first two nonconstant coefficients of this expansion explicitly.

</details>


### [192] [The Cartan-Hadamard conjecture in dimension five](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11005)
*Shibing Chen,Mohammad Ghomi,Peng Wang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明了在五维完备单连通非正截面曲率黎曼流形中，欧氏等周不等式仍然成立，从而在该维度解决了 Cartan-Hadamard 猜想。核心工具是常平均曲率超曲面的一个精确不等式，并结合等周轮廓论证完成证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cartan-Hadamard 猜想断言非正截面曲率流形中的等周不等式与欧氏空间一致，此前仅在低维（如三维）成立。本文旨在推进并解决五维情形，填补该猜想在更高维度的空白。

Method: 首先证明常平均曲率超曲面的一个精确不等式，其证明借助沿边界点对的积分（Banchoff-Pohl 思想）以及对测地弦上 Jacobi 场的估计。该不等式可推广至测地球中等周区域的边界，此时平均曲率仅在自由部分为常数。随后采用 Kleiner 的等周轮廓论证完成整体证明。

Result: 在五维完备单连通非正截面曲率黎曼流形中，欧氏等周不等式成立，Cartan-Hadamard 猜想在该维度获得解决。所给方法同时给出了三维情形的一个新证明。

Conclusion: 本文通过常平均曲率超曲面的精确不等式与等周轮廓论证，解决了五维的 Cartan-Hadamard 猜想，并为三维情形提供了新证明，展示了该方法在等周问题中的有效性。

Abstract: We show that the sharp Euclidean isoperimetric inequality holds for domains in complete simply connected Riemannian $5$-manifolds of nonpositive sectional curvature, which establishes the Cartan-Hadamard conjecture in that dimension. The main step is a sharp inequality for constant-mean-curvature hypersurfaces, proved via integrals over pairs of boundary points, in the spirit of Banchoff-Pohl, together with an estimate for Jacobi fields along geodesic chords. The inequality persists for boundaries of isoperimetric regions in geodesic balls, whose mean curvature is constant only on the free part. An isoperimetric-profile argument, after Kleiner, completes the proof. Our method also gives a new proof in dimension $3$.

</details>


### [193] [On static manifolds with boundary admitting a nowhere-vanishing static potential](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11349)
*Vladimir Medvedev*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 研究带边完备静态流形，在标量曲率与边界平均曲率的自然下界假设下，证明简单静态流形必具有正标量曲率、负边界平均曲率且紧致，并给出体积与边界几何的显式关系与估计；在标量平坦情形得到全局分裂与Ricci平坦刚性结果（含不连通边界），在负标量曲率情形建立尖锐平均曲率界，并用指数warped-product结构刻画等号情形。证明本质上依赖于对关联Einstein流形的研究，附录给出若干恒等式并讨论无边情形下的关联Einstein流形技巧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究带边静态流形在标量曲率与边界平均曲率自然下界下的整体几何与刚性，补充和推广既有（多为无边情形）的静态流形理论。

Method: 核心是研究关联的Einstein流形（associated Einstein manifold）及相应的恒等式；结合warped-product结构分析、边界几何估计与等号情形刻画。

Result: 简单静态流形必为正标量曲率、负边界平均曲率且紧致；获得体积与边界几何的显式关系与估计；标量平坦情形得到全局分裂与Ricci平坦刚性（含不连通边界）；负标量曲率情形得到尖锐平均曲率界，等号情形由指数warped-product结构刻画。

Conclusion: 带边静态流形的几何受标量曲率—边界平均曲率下界强约束，关联Einstein流形方法是证明的关键工具，并可在无边情形下延用；附录恒等式为进一步研究提供基础。

Abstract: We study complete static manifolds with boundary admitting a nowhere-vanishing static potential. Our main result shows that, under a natural lower bound relating the scalar curvature and the boundary mean curvature, a simple static manifold with boundary must in fact have positive scalar curvature, negative boundary mean curvature, and be compact; we also obtain explicit relations and estimates involving the volume of the manifold and the geometry of its boundary. In the scalar-flat case, we prove global splitting and Ricci-flat rigidity results, including for disconnected boundary, while in the negative scalar curvature case we establish a sharp mean-curvature bound and characterize the equality case by an exponential warped-product structure. The proofs rely essentially on the study of the associated Einstein manifold. In appendix we derive several identities for static manifolds with boundary and discuss the associated Einstein manifold technique in the boundaryless setting.

</details>


### [194] [Non-strict negativity of holomorphic bisectional curvature for compact relative Kähler fibrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11388)
*Xueyuan Wan*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文否定了To-Yeung关于全纯双截面曲率严格负性在紧致全纯纤维化中从底空间与纤维传递到全空间的开问题，并进一步说明参数空间上的广义Weil-Petersson度量也无法保证混合双截面曲率的严格负性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨紧致全纯纤维化中全纯双截面曲率严格负性是否可从底空间与纤维传递到全空间，特别是在Kodaira-Spencer映射处处单射（即有效形变）的条件下，回答To与Yeung提出的开问题。

Method: 构造一个紧致相对Kähler纤维化：底为亏格二的曲线，全空间为光滑射影三维簇，Kodaira-Spencer映射处处单射，诱导纤维度量具有严格负的全纯双截面曲率，但全空间不存在具备该曲率性质的Kähler度量。另构造来自Shimura曲线上通用四元数阿贝尔曲面的紧致、有效参数化的Monge-Ampère纤维化，并研究其乘积族的广义Weil-Petersson度量。

Result: 族的总空间不存在具有严格负全纯双截面曲率的Kähler度量，故严格负性不必从底空间与纤维传递到全空间；对乘积族，广义Weil-Petersson度量具非正全纯双截面曲率与严格负全纯截面曲率，但混合双截面曲率恒为零。

Conclusion: 严格双截面负性在存在性层面（全空间度量）与自然度量层面（参数空间上的Weil-Petersson度量）均可失效，即便每一点处形变有效，从而对To-Yeung问题给出否定回答。

Abstract: Strict negativity of holomorphic bisectional curvature need not pass from the base and fibers of a compact holomorphic fibration to its total space, even when the Kodaira-Spencer map is everywhere injective. We construct a compact relative Kähler fibration over a genus-two curve, with a smooth projective threefold as total space and an everywhere injective Kodaira--Spencer map, whose induced fiber metrics have strictly negative holomorphic bisectional curvature, whereas the total space admits no Kähler metric with this curvature property. This gives a negative answer to an open problem posed by To and Yeung. We also construct compact, effectively parametrized Monge-Ampère fibrations from universal quaternionic abelian surfaces over Shimura curves. For products of these families, the generalized Weil-Petersson metric has nonpositive holomorphic bisectional curvature and strictly negative holomorphic sectional curvature, but its mixed bisectional curvatures vanish. Thus strict bisectional negativity can fail both for the existence of a metric on the total space and for the natural metric on the parameter space, despite effective variation at every point.

</details>


### [195] [Optimal geometric inequalities and fully nonlinear conformal flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11421)
*Yuxin Ge,Guofang Wang,Wei Wei*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文在球面 $\mathbb{S}^n$ 上建立了涉及总 $\sigma_k$-曲率 $\int_{\mathbb{S}^n}\sigma_k(g)\,dv_g$ 的 sharp Sobolev 型几何不等式，将 Guan–Wang 的最优不等式从锥 $\mathcal{C}_k$ 推广到更大的严格锥 $\mathcal{C}_{k-1}$，并通过完全非线性共形流变分方法在 $\mathcal{C}_{k-1}$ 中进行处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Guan–Wang 先前的最优不等式仅适用于锥 $\mathcal{C}_k$，限制了对 admissible conformal metrics 的范围。作者希望将其推广到严格更大的锥 $\mathcal{C}_{k-1}$，以扩大 admissible 共形度量的范围，并解决相关维数下的公开问题。

Method: 采用变分方法，并通过一个完全非线性共形流实现。在 $\mathcal{C}_{k-1}$ 中工作时面临实质性解析困难：需在同时验证流保持抛物性的前提下获得 $C^2$ 先验估计。作者通过精心设计的 test function，并对 Hessian 矩阵的最大特征值应用 maximum principle 来解决这些问题。

Result: 在 $\mathbb{S}^n$ 上建立了涉及总 $\sigma_k$-曲率的 sharp Sobolev 型几何不等式，成功将 Guan–Wang 的最优不等式从 $\mathcal{C}_k$ 推广至 $\mathcal{C}_{k-1}$；获得所需的 $C^2$ a priori estimates，并证明流保持抛物性。作为应用，解决了 3 维和 4 维中的两个公开问题。最后给出例子说明这些不等式不能推广至 $\mathcal{C}_{k-2}$。

Conclusion: 该工作将 sharp Sobolev 型几何不等式的最优 admissible 范围从 $\mathcal{C}_k$ 拓展到 $\mathcal{C}_{k-1}$，同时通过 test function 与 maximum principle 技术克服了完全非线性共形流中的解析困难；$\mathcal{C}_{k-2}$ 情形被反例排除，从而明确刻画了不等式的适用范围边界。

Abstract: We establish sharp Sobolev-type geometric inequalities on $\mathbb{S}^n$ involving the total $σ_k$-curvatures $\int_{\mathbb{S}^n}σ_k(g)\,dv_g$. These results extend the optimal inequalities of Guan--Wang~\cite{GWDuke} from the cone $\mathcal{C}_k$ to the strictly larger cone $\mathcal{C}_{k-1}$, thereby enlarging the range of admissible conformal metrics. Our approach is variational and is implemented through a fully nonlinear conformal flow. Working in $\mathcal{C}_{k-1}$ introduces substantial analytic difficulties; in particular, one must obtain $C^2$ a priori estimates while simultaneously verifying that the flow remains parabolic. We resolve these issues via a carefully designed test function and by applying the maximum principle to the maximal eigenvalue of the Hessian matrix. As applications, we solve two open problems in dimensions 3 and 4. Finally, we give examples to show that these inequalities cannot be extended to $\mathcal{C}_{k-2}$.

</details>


### [196] [A Seifert Dichotomy for Z2-Harmonic One-Forms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11429)
*Weifeng Sun*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究Seifert纤维化有理同调三维球面上Z/2-调和1-形式的存在性，建立了二分性：至多三条例外纤维时在纤维充分塌缩的联络-度量族下不存在；至少四条例外纤维时对任意黎曼度量存在。并完成了非可定向基的相应分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刻画Z/2-调和1-形式在Seifert纤维化有理同调三维球面上的存在性与不存在性，明确例外纤维数目的临界行为，并补全非可定向基情形下的分类。

Method: 在定向基情形下，限制于至多三条例外纤维，沿固定联络-度量族在纤维充分塌缩的极限下证明不存在性；对至少四条例外纤维，引用已有结果得到对任意黎曼度量的存在性；并针对非可定向基完成相应分类。

Result: 得到Z/2-调和1-形式的二分性：至多三条例外纤维时，在纤维充分塌缩的固定联络-度量族下不存在；至少四条例外纤维时，对每个黎曼度量均存在；同时给出非可定向基的完整分类。

Conclusion: 例外纤维数量是决定Z/2-调和1-形式存在性的关键阈值，存在性在纤维塌缩极限下发生转变，且该二分现象可推广至非可定向基。

Abstract: We establish a dichotomy for Z/2-harmonic one-forms on Seifert fibered rational homology three-spheres. For an oriented base with at most three exceptional fibers, we prove nonexistence along any fixed connection-metric family when the fibers are sufficiently collapsed. With at least four exceptional fibers, existing results give existence for every Riemannian metric. We also complete the corresponding classification for nonorientable bases.

</details>


### [197] [Stability and Area-Minimizing Property of Higher-Dimensional Helicoids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11481)
*Chung-Jun Tsai,Mao-Pei Tsui,Jingbo Wan,Mu-Tao Wang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 研究高维螺旋面 $H_k\\subset\\mathbb{R}^{2k+1}$ 的稳定性与极小面积性质：$k\\geq 3$ 稳定、$k\\leq 2$ 不稳定；$k\\geq 3$ 时偶数 $k$ 为面积最小化，奇数 $k$ 不是。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典螺旋面（$\\mathbb{R}^3$ 中的 $k=1$）是极小曲面的重要范例，其高维类比 $H_k$ 构成一类完整、真嵌入、微分同胚于 $\\mathbb{R}^{k+1}$ 的极小子流形族。作者希望确定该族在一般维数下的稳定性与面积最小化性质，以理解维数升高对极小子流形行为的影响。

Method: 将 $H_k$ 参数化为 $(u_1e^{is},\\ldots,u_ke^{is},s)$，刻画 $(k+1)$ 维螺旋面。通过分析其 Jacobi 算子（二阶变分）判定稳定性；对 $k\\geq 3$ 的偶数情形，构造显式 calibration 证明面积最小化；对奇数情形，通过构造显式竞争曲面证明非面积最小化。

Result: 完全确定稳定性：$H_k$ 对 $k\\geq 3$ 稳定，对 $k\\leq 2$ 不稳定，临界转折在 $k=3$——四维螺旋面 $H_3\\subset\\mathbb{R}^7$ 已稳定，而经典螺旋面 $(k=1)$ 及其首个高维类比 $(k=2)$ 不稳定。对 $k\\geq 3$，$k$ 偶数时面积最小化，$k$ 奇数时非面积最小化。特别地，每个偶数 $k\\geq 4$，$H_k$ 是 $\\mathbb{R}^{2k+1}$ 中的整极小图且面积最小化。

Conclusion: 高维螺旋面 $H_k$ 的稳定性与面积最小化性质随维数呈现清晰规律，$k=3$ 处出现显著转折；偶数维情形给出整极小图且面积最小化的新例子。

Abstract: For each integer $k\geq 1$, we study the $(k+1)$-dimensional helicoid $H_k\subset\mathbb{R}^{2k+1}$ parametrized by \[
  (u_1,\ldots,u_k,s) \longmapsto \bigl(u_1e^{is},\ldots,u_ke^{is},s\bigr) \in \mathbb{C}^k\times\mathbb{R}\cong \mathbb{R}^{2k+1}. \] These helicoids form a basic and distinguished family of complete, properly embedded minimal submanifolds diffeomorphic to $\mathbb{R}^{k+1}$, and provide natural higher-dimensional analogues of the classical helicoid in $\mathbb{R}^3$.
  We completely determine their stability: $H_k$ is stable for $k\geq 3$ and unstable for $k\leq 2$. The sharp transition at $k=3$ is particularly striking: while the classical helicoid $(k=1)$ and its first higher-dimensional analogue $(k=2)$ are unstable, the four-dimensional helicoid $H_3\subset\mathbb{R}^7$ is already stable.
  For $k\geq 3$, we also determine their area-minimizing property: $H_k$ is area-minimizing when $k$ is even and not area-minimizing when $k$ is odd. The area-minimizing result is proved by constructing an explicit calibration, while the non-area-minimizing result follows from an explicit competitor. In particular, for every even $k\geq 4$, the $(k+1)$-dimensional helicoid $H_k$ is an entire minimal graph in $\mathbb{R}^{2k+1}$ that is area-minimizing.

</details>


### [198] [A positively curved metric on the Gromoll-Meyer sphere](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11484)
*Zexuan Ouyang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文构造了Sp(2)上双不变度量的显式形变，使Gromoll-Meyer异种7-球面上诱导度量在小正参数下具有正截面曲率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Gromoll-Meyer异种7-球面能否赋予正截面曲率度量，是一个长期关注的微分几何问题。

Method: 利用与商作用相容的固定自伴算子构造双不变度量的形变；结合一致曲率估计与水平交换平面的代数分析；在低阶正项消失的临界集上，给出曲率分子三次系数显式正下界；并通过四阶余项的一致控制与紧性论证完成证明。

Result: 证明了该形变诱导的度量在所有充分小的正参数下，Gromoll-Meyer异种7-球面具有正截面曲率。

Conclusion: Gromoll-Meyer异种7-球面可赋予正截面曲率度量，为异种球面正曲率度量存在性问题提供了新例证。

Abstract: We construct an explicit deformation of the bi-invariant metric on Sp(2) whose induced metrics on the Gromoll-Meyer exotic 7-sphere have positive sectional curvature for all sufficiently small positive parameters. The deformation is determined by a fixed self-adjoint operator compatible with the quotient action. The proof combines a uniform curvature estimate with an algebraic analysis of the horizontal commuting planes. On the critical set where the lower-order positive terms vanish, we establish an explicit positive lower bound for the cubic coefficient of the curvature numerator. Uniform control of the fourth-order remainder and a compactness argument then show that every sectional curvature is positive

</details>


### [199] [A Complex Structure on A Rational Homology Six-Sphere with Two-Torsion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11541)
*Zichang Wang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 构造了一个紧致、光滑、单连通的复三维流形，其第二、第三整同调群为二阶循环群，其余同调群与六维球面一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索复三维流形在同调层面逼近六维球面 S^6 的可能性，构造具有特殊同调（含挠部分）的紧致光滑单连通复三维流形。

Method: 通过复几何/代数几何构造方法，显式构造一个紧致、光滑、单连通的复三维流形，并计算其整同调群。

Result: 所得复三维流形为紧致、光滑、单连通；H_2 与 H_3 为 Z/2（二阶循环群），其余整同调群与六维球面 S^6 相同。

Conclusion: 存在紧致、光滑、单连通复三维流形，其整同调除二阶挠（H_2、H_3 ≅ Z/2）外与 S^6 一致，展示了 S^6 同调型在复三维流形中的可实现在挠部分的表现。

Abstract: We construct a compact, smooth, simply connected complex threefold. Its second and third integral homology groups are cyclic of order two, and its remaining homology groups agree with those of the six-sphere.

</details>


### [200] [Non-trivial ABC-Massey products on complex parallelisable solvmanifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11597)
*Nunzia Cesarino,Tommaso Sferruzza,Adriano Tomassini*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究三重 ABC-Massey 积（Bott-Chern 与 Aeppli 上同调上的高阶运算），证明其非零性在非阿贝尔复幺模可解李代数中恒成立，并推广到紧复可平行化 solvmanifold。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三重 ABC-Massey 积的非零性是平坦（pluripotential）同伦型不变量，可用于区分几何结构。作者希望刻画哪些复流形/李代数必然产生此类非平凡高阶运算。

Method: 在紧复流形的 Bott-Chern 与 Aeppli 上同调上定义三重 ABC-Massey 积；对非阿贝尔复幺模可解李代数作出构造性证明，并通过紧复可平行化 solvmanifold 与其李代数的对应关系进行推广。

Result: 证明每个非阿贝尔复幺模可解李代数都存在非零的三重 ABC-Massey 积；由此推出每个紧复可平行化 solvmanifold 也具有非零三重 ABC-Massey 积。

Conclusion: 非阿贝尔复幺模可解李代数及紧复可平行化 solvmanifold 均携带非平凡三重 ABC-Massey 积，说明这类几何普遍存在非平凡的高阶上同调运算，并可作为同伦型不变量的判据。

Abstract: Triple Aeppli--Bott--Chern--Massey products, shortly, triple ABC-Massey products are higher-order operations on the Bott--Chern and Aeppli cohomologies of a compact complex manifold, and their non-vanishing is an invariant of its pluripotential homotopy type. We prove that every non-Abelian complex unimodular solvable Lie algebra admits a non-vanishing triple ABC--Massey product, and we deduce that so does every compact complex parallelisable solvmanifold.

</details>


### [201] [Spectral complexes and currents on the Engel group](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11625)
*Filippa Lo Biundo,Francesca Tripaldi*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 研究Engel群上de Rham复形作为截断多重复形所产生的谱复形，刻画其紧支版本，定义谱流，并证明光滑谱复形嵌入流复形；进一步证明$W_{\mathrm{loc}}^{1,q}$（$q>7$）映射的Pansu拉回诱导谱流复形间的态射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在次黎曼几何中，Engel群是典型的非交换、非球面、具有次黎曼结构的幂零李群。传统de Rham复形在其上难以直接沿用，而将其视为截断多重复形可自然产生谱复形。作者希望建立这些谱复形的解析框架（紧支、对偶、嵌入）并研究其在低正则性映射下的函子性，以推广次黎曼流形上的de Rham理论及Pansu拉回理论。

Method: 将Engel群$G$上的de Rham复形视为截断多重复形，从而构造两个相应的谱复形；分析其紧支对应物；通过对偶定义谱流；证明光滑谱复形自然嵌入流复形；对$W_{\mathrm{loc}}^{1,q}$（$q>7$）映射$\varphi\colon G\to G$，研究其Pansu拉回并证明其诱导谱流复形之间的态射。

Result: 刻画了由截断多重复形产生的两个谱复形及其紧支版本；通过定义对偶构造了谱流；证明了光滑谱复形到流复形的自然嵌入；并证明了当$q>7$时，$W_{\mathrm{loc}}^{1,q}$映射的Pansu拉回可诱导谱流复形间的态射。

Conclusion: Engel群上的截断多重复形给出了一套自然的谱复形与谱流理论；光滑复形可嵌入流复形；在阈值$q>7$下，低正则映射的Pansu拉回保持了该复形结构，从而为次黎曼de Rham理论与Pansu拉回提供了函子性结果。

Abstract: We study the spectral complexes arising from the de Rham complex on the Engel group $G$ viewed as a truncated multicomplex. We describe the two resulting complexes, analyse their compactly supported counterparts, and define the corresponding spectral currents by duality. We prove that the smooth spectral complexes embed naturally into the current complexes. Finally, we show that the Pansu pullback of a $W_{\mathrm{loc}}^{1,q}$ map $\varphi\colon G\to G$ with $q>7$ induces a morphism into the corresponding complexes of spectral currents.

</details>


### [202] [A Thorpe Trick for the Bochner Technique](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11653)
*Matthias Wink*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 证明高维闭可定向黎曼流形上（⌈n/2⌉+ε）-正曲率算子蕴含实同调球，n=6时可改进为4-正曲率算子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究正曲率算子条件与流形同调类型之间的关系，寻求在低于经典"半维曲率算子"阈值下仍能得到实同调球这一强拓扑结论。

Method: 采用黎曼几何中曲率算子的部分正性（k-正曲率算子）概念，通过对曲率算子谱的定量控制与Bochner型技术证明同调消失。

Result: 对n≥6，存在ε(n)>0使得任何（⌈n/2⌉+ε）-正曲率算子的闭可定向黎曼流形为实同调球；对n=6，4-正曲率算子即已足够。

Conclusion: 部分正曲率算子的临界阈值可低于⌈n/2⌉（n=6为4），仍强制流形为实同调球。

Abstract: We prove that for $n \geq 6$ there exists $\varepsilon (n)>0$ such that every closed orientable Riemannian manifold with $\left( \lceil \frac{n}{2} \rceil+ \varepsilon\right)$-positive curvature operator is a real homology sphere. For $n=6$ we show that the same result holds for manifolds with $4$-positive curvature operators.

</details>


### [203] [A vanishing result for harmonic $(n-1,1)$-forms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11665)
*Matthias Wink*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 证明闭Kähler流形上本原调和(n-1,1)-形式在Kähler曲率算子满足特定正性条件下消失，并由此刻画实上同调复射影空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Kähler流形的曲率正性与调和形式消失性之间的关系，推广经典曲率-拓扑刚性结果。

Method: 利用曲率算子正性条件下的Bochner型Weitzenböck公式分析，对本原(n-1,1)-形式进行积分估计。

Result: 复维数n≥4时，Kähler曲率算子((n^2-n+2)/2)-正蕴含本原(n-1,1)-形式消失；n=3时为7/2-正。n≥3且Kähler曲率算子3-正时，为实上同调复射影空间。

Conclusion: 曲率算子正性可强制特定类型调和形式消失，从而推出流形的实上同调刚性结论。

Abstract: We prove that every primitive harmonic $(n-1,1)$-form of a closed Kähler manifold of complex dimension $n$ vanishes provided its Kähler curvature operator is $\frac{n^2-n+2}{2}$-positive if $n \geq 4$, respectively $\frac{7}{2}$-positive if $n=3$. As a consequence, a closed Kähler manifold of complex dimension $n \geq 3$ with $3$-positive Kähler curvature operator is a real cohomology complex projective space.

</details>


### [204] [Rigidity and flexibility under positive isotropic curvature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11702)
*Tsz-Kiu Aaron Chow,Yipeng Wang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该文构造了 $S^n$ 上具有任意大 Urysohn $1$-宽度及大内半径稳定极小圆盘的 $4$-PIC 度量，从而否定了正迷向曲率下界情形下的宽度与稳定圆盘半径界猜想；同时在闭偶维流形上证明了 $\sigma$-PIC 下 $\lambda_1^{(2)}\ge(n-1)\sigma/2$ 的 sharp 估计，并给出等号情形下的圆性刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在正迷向曲率（PIC）下界条件下，此前有猜想认为 Urysohn 宽度与稳定极小圆盘的内半径应受曲率下界控制。本文旨在检验这些猜想的正确性，并进一步研究 $\sigma$-PIC 下 $2$-形式 Laplacian 第一特征值的 sharp 下界及其刚性。

Method: 对每个 $n\ge4$ 与 $L>0$，显式构造 $S^n$ 上的光滑 $4$-PIC 度量，并估计其 Urysohn $1$-宽度与所嵌稳定极小圆盘的内半径；在闭偶维流形上，利用 $\sigma$-PIC 条件下的 Bochner 型论证证明 $\lambda_1^{(2)}$ 的 sharp 下界，并分析等号情形下达到该界的闭特征形式若在某点秩至少为四则强制圆性。

Result: 构造出的 $4$-PIC 度量使 Urysohn $1$-宽度和内半径任意大，否定了正迷向曲率下界下所预期的宽度与稳定圆盘半径界。另一方面，在闭偶维流形上证明了 sharp 估计 $\lambda_1^{(2)}\ge(n-1)\sigma/2$，并证明当达到该界的闭特征形式在某点秩至少为四时等号强制流形为圆球。

Conclusion: Urysohn 宽度与稳定极小圆盘半径在 $4$-PIC 条件下并不受迷向曲率下界的一致控制，相关猜想不成立；而 $\sigma$-PIC 下 $2$-形式特征值的 sharp 下界成立，且等号在特定秩条件下蕴含圆性。

Abstract: For every $n\ge4$ and $L>0$, we construct a smooth $4$-PIC metric on $S^n$ with Urysohn $1$-width at least $L$ and an embedded stable minimal disk of intrinsic inradius at least $L$. These examples disprove the proposed width and stable-disk radius bounds under a positive lower bound for isotropic curvature. On closed even-dimensional manifolds, we prove the sharp estimate $λ_1^{(2)}\ge(n-1)σ/2$ under $σ$-PIC and show that equality forces roundness if a closed eigenform attaining the bound has rank at least four at some point.

</details>


### [205] [The Rigidity of the closed three dimensional regular Landsberg metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11718)
*Jianyu Mao,Linfeng Zhou*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该文证明：闭三维流形上任何光滑强凸正则Landsberg度量必为Berwald度量，从而在三维闭流形情形下解决了Landsberg-Berwald猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Landsberg-Berwald猜想长期未决，核心是判断正则Landsberg度量是否一定为Berwald度量，即在闭三维流形上是否存在"正则Landsberg独角兽"。

Method: 证明结合了三项工具：闭曲面上交换Codazzi三次张量的消没定理、三维Minkowski范数在常曲率指标曲面下的刚性定理，以及非线性曲率的秩一论证；剩余的R-二次情形通过沿测地流的紧致性处理。

Result: 在闭三维流形上，每个光滑强凸正则Landsberg度量都是Berwald度量，无需可逆性假设；即闭三维流形上不存在正则Landsberg"独角兽"。

Conclusion: 该结果在闭三维流形情形下解决了Landsberg-Berwald猜想；纤维常曲率定理仅作为论证中的工具，并未断言完整的Laugwitz猜想。

Abstract: The Landsberg Berwald conjecture asks whether every regular Landsberg metric is Berwald. We settle this conjecture for closed three-dimensional manifolds: every smooth strongly convex regular Landsberg metric on such a manifold is Berwald, without any reversibility assumption. Equivalently, there are no regular Landsberg "unicorns" on closed three-manifolds; this is a reformulation of the same conjecture, rather than a second independent conjecture.The proof combines a vanishing theorem for commuting Codazzi cubic tensors on closed surfaces, a rigidity theorem for three dimensional Minkowski norms with constant curvature indicatrices, and a rank one argument for the nonlinear curvature. The remaining R-quadratic case is settled by compactness along the geodesic flow. The fibrewise constant curvature theorem is an ingredient in this argument and does not assert the full Laugwitz conjecture.

</details>


### [206] [Local classification of chsc Kähler metrics with cone singularities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11760)
*Martin de Borbon*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 证明一个在挖去超平面的球上、沿超平面多齐次且与角为2πβ的锥度量一致等价的常全纯截面曲率Kähler度量，必为相应复空间形式经分枝覆盖映射的拉回。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究具有常全纯截面曲率（chsc）的Kähler度量在锥奇点（沿复超平面D）附近的刚性/分类性质。经典复空间形式在光滑情形下由chsc完全决定，本文希望刻画沿超平面带有锥奇异且呈多齐次渐近展开的这一类度量是否仍被其奇点模型唯一确定。

Method: 设B为原点处球，D={z^1=0}。考虑B\D上chsc Kähler度量g，要求它（i）与角2πβ（0<β<1）的模型锥度量一致等价（uniformly equivalent），（ii）沿D多齐次（polyhomogeneous）。在此解析假设下，通过构造以原点为中心的合适全纯坐标(w^1,…,w^n)，分析沿D的奇点阶数与多齐次展开的系数递推，论证g的结构被锥角与曲率唯一锁定。

Result: 证明存在以原点为中心的全纯坐标(w^1,…,w^n)，使得度量g恰好等于模型复空间形式经分枝覆盖映射 (w^1,w^2,…,w^n) ↦ ((w^1)^β, w^2,…,w^n) 的拉回。即g在奇点附近与由该β次幂映射诱导的度量的拉回完全重合。

Conclusion: 在一致等价于角2πβ锥度量、且沿D多齐次的假设下，球上挖去超平面的chsc Kähler度量被完全分类为相应复空间形式的β次分枝覆盖拉回，说明锥奇点情形下的刚性成立。

Abstract: Let $B\subset\mathbb{C}^n$ be a ball centred at the origin and $D=\{z^1=0\}$. Let $g$ be a Kähler metric of constant holomorphic sectional curvature (chsc) on $B\setminus D$, uniformly equivalent to the model cone metric of angle $2πβ$, with $0<β<1$, and polyhomogeneous along $D$. We prove that, in suitable holomorphic coordinates $(w^1, \ldots, w^n)$ centred at the origin, the metric $g$ is the pullback of the corresponding complex space form by the map $(w^1,w^2,\dots,w^n)\longmapsto \bigl((w^1)^β,w^2,\dots,w^n\bigr)$.

</details>


### [207] [Integral Identities and Rigidity of Generalized $m$-Quasi-Einstein Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11820)
*Alcides de Carvalho,W. O. Costa-Filho*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文建立闭广义m-拟爱因斯坦流形的微分与积分恒等式，据此给出共形性、Killing条件与平凡性判据，并在m=-2和m=-4处证明Colling–Dunajski猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为统一若干刚性现象提供框架，并解决Colling–Dunajski在m=-2（常λ≤0）与m=-4处的猜想，同时将m≤-n的已知平凡性结果推广到广义m-拟爱因斯坦情形。

Method: 建立闭广义m-拟爱因斯坦流形的微分与积分恒等式；将积分Bochner公式改写为Witten型Hodge-能量恒等式，利用m=-2处四次项消去；对漂移Laplace算子给出恒等式；在常λ情形导出扭曲算子的微分恒等式以锁定m=-4；结合谱与极值原理进行论证。

Result: 得到共形、Killing与平凡性判据；在自然符号条件下于m=-2证明平凡性定理，特别在常λ≤0时证明Colling–Dunajski猜想；给出m≤-n已知平凡性结果的新证明并推广；证明m=-4处Colling–Dunajski猜想；附录给出势场为共形但非Killing的闭广义m-拟爱因斯坦流形实例。

Conclusion: 所建立的恒等式框架统一了多种刚性现象，并证明Colling–Dunajski猜想在m=-2与m=-4处成立，同时表明共形势场未必是Killing的。

Abstract: We establish differential and integral identities for closed generalized $m$-quasi-Einstein manifolds $(M^n,g,X,λ)$. These identities yield criteria for conformality, the Killing condition, and triviality, and provide a unified framework for several rigidity phenomena. Afther this, we recast the integrated Bochner formula as a Witten-type Hodge-energy identity. The cancellation of its quartic term at $m=-2$ leads to a triviality theorem in the generalized setting under a natural sign condition and, in particular, proves a recently Colling--Dunajski conjecture at $m=-2$ when $λ\leq0$ is constant.
  An identity for the drift laplacian gives a new proof of the previously known triviality result for $m\leq-n$ and extends it to generalized $m$-quasi-Einstein manifolds. In the constant-$λ$ case, we then derive a differential identities for twisted operators that singles out the value $m=-4$. Combined with spectral and maximum-principle arguments, this yields triviality for $λ\leq0$, thereby proving the Colling--Dunajski conjecture at $m=-4$. Finally, complementing our criterion characterizing when a conformal potential field is Killing, we exhibit in the appendix a closed generalized \(m\)-quasi-Einstein manifold whose potential field is conformal but non-Killing.

</details>

# math.QA [[Back]](#toc)

### [208] [On Diagrammatic Categorification of Verma Modules I: Braiding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10941)
*Pedro Guicardi*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 本文将KLRW代数推广到$\mathfrak{sl}_2$ Verma模张量积表示，构造了R-矩阵的范畴化函子及其投射消解，证明辫群作用，并建立辫补上的Khovanov同调，猜想其与Annular Khovanov同调维数相同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构造$S^3$中纽结补的Khovanov同调理论，并范畴化纽结补的Gukov-Manolescu二变量级数，此工作分两部分完成。

Method: 将KLRW代数推广至$\mathfrak{sl}_2$ Verma模张量积表示；以图式双模的导出张量积构造R-矩阵范畴化函子，显式计算其投射消解；证明辫合函子诱导辫群作用；引入有限维表示中的线股以定义辫补上的Khovanov同调函子。

Result: 构造出Verma模R-矩阵的范畴化函子及投射消解，获得辫群作用；对unknot情形得到$S^1\times D^2$中的纽结同调，并通过若干例子与Annular Khovanov同调比较，发现二者密切相关，猜想维数相同。

Conclusion: 提出Verma模着色线股的cup与cap范畴化方案，为后续论文奠定基础。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the extensions of KLRW algebras to tensor products of Verma module representations of $\mathfrak{sl}_2$. Our motivation is to construct a theory of Khovanov homology for knot complements in $S^3$ (and which also categorifies the Gukov-Manolescu two-variable series for knot complements), which will be done in the second part of this work. We construct the categorification of R-matrices for Verma modules as functors given by derived tensor products with diagrammatic bimodules and explicitly compute their projective resolutions. We also prove these braiding functors induce an action of the braid group on the relevant categories. Then, we describe how to incorporate strands in finite-dimensional representations of $\mathfrak{sl}_2$, thereby establishing functors that serve as the Khovanov homology on a braid complement. In the case of the unknot, this gives knot homologies in $S^1\times D^2$, which we compare to Annular Khovanov Homology through several examples and show they are very closely related, conjecturing they are of the same dimension. We conclude with a proposal for the categorification of the cups and caps of Verma module colored strands, which we build upon in the next paper.

</details>


### [209] [Some finite dimensional representations of shifted quantum affine algebras of type A](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11337)
*Ryosuke Kodera,Kentaro Wada*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 研究A型shifted量子仿射代数的有限维表示，在分离条件下显式描述量子圈代数简单赋值模张量积与一维表示的关系，并给出部分有限维单模的q-特征标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: shifted量子仿射代数的有限维表示结构尚缺乏显式刻画，特别是其单模的q-特征标（q-characters）难以直接确定。本文旨在通过已知的量子圈代数简单赋值模与该代数的一维表示之间的张量积关系，为刻画其有限维单模提供具体途径。

Method: 针对A型shifted量子仿射代数，选取量子圈代数的简单赋值模（simple evaluation modules），在满足分离条件（separation condition）的前提下，显式地描述这些模与该shifted量子仿射代数的一维表示的张量积。

Result: 在分离条件下给出了量子圈代数简单赋值模与一维表示张量积的显式描述，并由此得到shifted量子仿射代数某些有限维简单模的q-特征标。

Conclusion: 通过对张量积的显式刻画，本文为A型shifted量子仿射代数有限维单模的q-特征标提供了一种具体构造方法，推动了该代数表示理论的进一步研究。

Abstract: In this paper, we study finite dimensional representations of shifted quantum affine algebras of type A. We give an explicit description of the tensor product of simple evaluation modules of the quantum loop algebra and a one-dimensional representation of the shifted quantum affine algebra under the separation condition. As a consequence, we give the q-characters of some finite dimensional simple modules of the shifted quantum affine algebra.

</details>


### [210] [Gelfand--Tsetlin bases for representations of $\mathrm U_{q}(\mathfrak{gl}_n)$ at roots of unity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11471)
*Hao Chang,Ruiying Hou,Jian Zhang*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 构造单位根处 U_q(gl_n) 表示的 Gelfand–Tsetlin 基，并应用于 U_q(gl_3) 的 baby Verma 模，给出显式基与合成列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单位根处的量子群表示（模表示理论）结构复杂，缺乏类似经典情形中便于计算的显式基；Gelfand–Tsetlin 基是研究 gl_n 表示组合结构的核心工具，其在根单位情形的推广具有自然的研究价值。

Method: 通过构造单位根处 U_q(gl_n) 表示的 Gelfand–Tsetlin 基（基于 Gelfand–Tsetlin 模式组合描述），并将该基应用于 U_q(gl_3) 的一类 baby Verma 模，计算其显式基。

Result: 得到 U_q(gl_n) 在单位根处表示的 Gelfand–Tsetlin 基；显式给出 U_q(gl_3) 某些 baby Verma 模的基，并确定其合成列。

Conclusion: Gelfand–Tsetlin 基的构造在单位根情形可行且有效，为研究 U_q(gl_3) baby Verma 模的不可约组成因子提供组合化工具，可望推广至更高秩。

Abstract: In this paper, we construct Gelfand--Tsetlin bases for representations of $\mathrm U_q(\mathfrak{gl}_n)$ at roots of unity. As an application, we obtain explicit bases for certain baby Verma modules of $\mathrm U_q(\mathfrak{gl}_3)$ and determine their composition series.

</details>


### [211] [Normalized quadratic extensions of pointed Hopf algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11622)
*Rongchuan Xiong*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 该论文给出有限维点Hopf代数指数二次扩张的内禀刻画，并用余代数Hochschild 2-余循环与Ore型数据分类这类扩张，进而分类特征2下特定16维非连通点Hopf代数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指数二次扩张是Hopf代数结构理论中的基本构造，但已有描述多依赖外部构造或特定情形，缺乏对一般有限维点Hopf代数的统一内禀刻画；同时低维非连通点Hopf代数的分类仍有空白。

Method: 设L为代数闭域上有限维点Hopf代数。研究满足H_0=L_0且dim H=2dim L的Hopf子代数L⊂H，证明H为L的规范化二次扩张；对固定支撑(g,h)，用余代数Hochschild 2-余循环和Ore型数据(σ,δ,u,v)描述扩张，并给出显式相容条件；再通过gauge变换与L的Hopf自同构轨道刻画同构类。

Result: 建立了指数二次扩张的内禀等价刻画：任何满足条件的Hopf代数H都是L的规范化二次扩张；对固定支撑(g,h)，扩张由余代数Hochschild 2-余循环及Ore型数据(σ,δ,u,v)决定，同构类由gauge变换与L的Hopf自同构的轨道参数化。作为应用，分类了特征2下维数16、具有一维无穷小braiding且其图不是Nichols代数的非连通点Hopf代数。

Conclusion: 该工作为点Hopf代数的二次扩张提供了统一的内禀框架，将扩张的分类归结为余代数Hochschild 2-余循环与Ore型数据的相容问题，并据此完成了一类16维非连通点Hopf代数的分类。

Abstract: Let $L$ be a finite-dimensional pointed Hopf algebra over an
  algebraically closed field. We establish an intrinsic characterization
  of index-two extensions: every Hopf algebra $H$ containing $L$ as a
  Hopf subalgebra and satisfying $H_0=L_0$ and
  $\dim H=2\dim L$ is a normalized quadratic extension of $L$.
  For a fixed support $(g,h)$, such extensions are described by a
  coalgebra Hochschild $2$-cocycle together with Ore-type data
  $(σ,δ,u,v)$ satisfying explicit compatibility conditions.
  Their isomorphism classes are parameterized by the orbits of gauge
  transformations and Hopf automorphisms of $L$. As an application, we
  classify non-connected pointed Hopf algebras of dimension $16$ in
  characteristic $2$ with one-dimensional infinitesimal braiding whose
  diagram is not a Nichols algebra.

</details>
